TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning in Memory (RiM)，一种用固定记忆块替代自回归生成推理步骤的潜在推理方法。记忆块作为特殊token序列，可在单次前向传播中处理，通过两阶段课程学习实现高效潜在推理，在多个推理基准上匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的测试时计算扩展将推理过程与自回归生成耦合，导致内部计算与外部通信混为一谈。受人类工作记忆机制的启发，作者希望实现一种无需将中间思考过程外部化的潜在推理方法。

Method: RiM使用固定序列的特殊token作为记忆块来解锁LLM的工作记忆容量。这些记忆块非自回归生成，可在单次前向传播中处理。采用两阶段课程：(1) 通过预测每个记忆块后的显式推理步骤进行grounding；(2) 丢弃步骤级监督，在每个记忆块后迭代精炼最终答案。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，RiM在不同家族和规模的语言模型中匹配或超越了现有潜在推理方法，同时避免了推理步骤的自回归生成。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可以通过训练使用工作记忆作为潜在推理的有效机制，实现计算高效的推理过程。

Abstract: To improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models, test-time compute is typically scaled by generating intermediate tokens before the final answer. However, this couples reasoning to autoregressive generation and thereby conflates internal computation with external communication. In contrast, human cognition can use working memory to hold and manipulate information internally without the need to externalize intermediate thoughts. Drawing on this principle, we introduce Reasoning in Memory (RiM), a latent reasoning method that replaces the autoregressive generation of reasoning steps with memory blocks. These memory blocks are fixed sequences of special tokens that unlock the working-memory capacity of large language models. Since they are fixed rather than generated, they can be processed in a single forward pass, enabling compute-efficient latent reasoning. To operationalize these memory blocks, we employ a two-stage curriculum. First, we ground them by predicting explicit reasoning steps after each memory block. Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.

</details>


### [113] [LLMSurgeon: Diagnosing Data Mixture of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30348)
*Yaxin Luo,Jiacheng Cui,Xiaohan Zhao,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Liu,Xinyue Bi,Zhaoyi Li,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出数据混合手术（DMS）框架LLMSurgeon，仅通过目标LLM的生成文本，在标签偏移假设下求解逆问题，估计其预训练语料的领域分布。通过校准的软混淆矩阵和约束逆问题，无需访问训练数据即可审计基础模型的"数字DNA"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的预训练数据混合构成其"数字DNA"，深刻影响模型行为、能力和失败模式，但该组成极少被公开披露，导致事后审计数据组合或来源困难。

Method: 将DMS形式化为标签偏移假设下的逆问题；估计校准的软混淆矩阵；通过求解约束逆问题来纠正系统性的领域混淆，恢复潜在混合先验。

Result: 在LLMScan评估套件上，LLMSurgeon在固定协议下能够高保真地恢复领域混合分布。

Conclusion: 提供了一种实用的事后审计方法，无需访问训练数据即可审计基础模型的数字DNA。

Abstract: The pretraining data mixture of Large Language Models (LLMs) constitutes their "digital DNA", shaping model behaviors, capabilities, and failure modes. Yet this composition is rarely disclosed, making post-hoc auditing of data combination or provenance difficult. In this work, we formalize $\textbf{Data Mixture Surgery (DMS)}$: given only generated text from a target LLM, estimate the domain-level distribution of its pretraining corpus under a predefined taxonomy. We propose $\textbf{LLMSurgeon}$, a strong framework that casts DMS as an inverse problem under the label-shift assumption. Rather than directly aggregating classifier outputs, LLMSurgeon estimates a calibrated $\textit{soft}$ confusion matrix and solves a constrained inverse problem to correct systematic domain confusion and recover the latent mixture prior. To evaluate, we introduce $\textbf{LLMScan}$, a recipe-verifiable evaluation suite built from open-source LLMs with transparent pretraining mixtures. Across LLMScan, LLMSurgeon recovers domain mixtures with high fidelity under fixed protocols. Our work presents a practical, post-hoc approach for auditing the digital DNA of foundation models without access to their training data.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Manifold-based Algorithms for the Hadamard Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28980)
*Nicolas Gillis,Subhayan Saha,Stefano Sicilia,Arnaud Vandaele*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hadamard分解（HD）问题，即寻找两个低秩矩阵X1（秩r1）和X2（秩r2），使得它们的元素级乘积近似等于给定矩阵X。文章提出了新的理论见解和三种高效算法，尤其适用于大规模稀疏数据，并在合成与真实数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HD相比标准低秩近似（如截断SVD）更具表达力，相同的参数数量下可以表示更高秩的矩阵（因为X1∘X2的秩通常为r1*r2）。需要开发高效算法来求解HD问题。

Method: 提出了三种新算法：(1) 基于Manopt工具箱的直接优化X≈X1∘X2；(2) 基于重写形式X≈WH^T的块投影梯度法；(3) 基于流形的梯度下降算法（无需投影到可行集）。同时提出了新的初始化策略。

Result: 数值实验表明新方法在合成数据和真实数据上均高效且具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 新算法有效且具有竞争力，最后两种算法特别适合大规模稀疏数据，新初始化策略提升了HD的精度。

Abstract: Given a matrix $X$, and two ranks $r_1$ and $r_2$, the Hadamard decomposition (HD) looks for two low-rank matrices, $X_1$ of rank $r_1$ and $X_2$ of rank $r_2$, both of the same size as $X$, such that $X\approx X_1\circ X_2$, where $\circ$ is the Hadamard (element-wise) product. In most cases, HD is more expressive than standard low-rank approximations such as the truncated singular value decomposition (TSVD), as it can represent higher-rank matrices with the same number of parameters; this is because the rank of $X_1 \circ X_2$ is generically equal to $r_1 r_2$. In this paper, we first present some theoretical insights for HD, in particular a useful reformulation $X\approx WH^\top$ where $W$ and $H$ have $r_1 r_2$ columns and belong to certain manifolds. These allow us to develop three new algorithms for computing HD. The first one uses the representation $X\approx X_1\circ X_2$ and relies on the Manopt toolbox. The other two rely on the reformulation $X\approx WH^\top$: one is a block projected gradient method, and the other is a manifold-based gradient descent algorithm that does not require projection onto the feasible set. The last two algorithms are particularly effective for handling large sparse data. We also propose new initializations that allow us to improve the accuracy of the HD. We compare our algorithms and initialization strategies with the TSVD and with the state of the art. Numerical results show that the new methods are efficient and competitive on both synthetic and real data.

</details>


### [115] [An existence result for a quantitative isoperimetric inequality in $\mathbb{R}^3$ involving the Hausdorff asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29046)
*Silvio Bove,Gisella Croce,Giovanni Pisante*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中包含 Hausdorff 不对称性的定量等周比 $\mathcal{Q}_*$ 的最优元存在性问题，证明了 $\mathcal{Q}_*$ 在固定体积的凸体类 $\mathcal{A}$ 上达到最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨包含 Hausdorff 不对称性的定量等周比是否存在最优元，即寻找在给定体积条件下使该比率最小的凸体形状。

Method: 通过数学分析的方法，在三维欧氏空间 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中，对固定体积的凸体类 $\mathcal{A}$ 进行研究，证明 $\mathcal{Q}_*$ 在该类上能够达到其最小值。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{Q}_*$ 在固定体积的凸体类 $\mathcal{A}$ 上确实存在最优元，即该定量等周比能够达到最小值。

Conclusion: 在三维空间中，包含 Hausdorff 不对称性的定量等周比在凸体类中具有最小值可达性。

Abstract: We study the existence of an optimizer for a quantitative isoperimetric ratio $\mathcal{Q}_*$ in $\mathbb{R}^3$ involving the Hausdorff asymmetry. We prove that $\mathcal{Q}_*$ attains its minimum over the class $\mathcal{A}$ of convex bodies of fixed volume.

</details>


### [116] [Restarted Accelerated Primal-Dual Algorithms with Adaptive Stepsizes for Nonlinear Conic Constrained Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29291)
*Necdet Serhat Aybat,Jinxin Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了适用于凸非线性锥规划（特别是二次约束二次规划）的带重启的加速原始-对偶算法（rAPDB），采用（非单调）回溯自适应步长策略，仅需一阶信息和矩阵-向量乘积，在KKT映射度量次正则性条件下证明了全局线性收敛，并通过数值实验验证了其在随机QCQP和核矩阵学习问题上的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原始-对偶方法主要针对双线性耦合问题设计，而凸非线性锥规划（如QCQP）产生的凸-凹极小极大重构中包含非双线性耦合项，导致现有方法不适用。本文旨在开发能处理此类非双线性耦合的一阶原始-对偶算法。

Method: 在加速原始-对偶自适应步长搜索方法基础上，设计了固定频率和自适应两种重启机制，并融入了单调与非单调自适应步长搜索策略。算法仅需一阶信息和矩阵-向量乘积，适合大规模和GPU加速实现。

Result: 在KKT映射度量次正则性条件下，证明了自中心光滑对偶间隙的二次增长性质，并建立了所提重启方法的全局线性收敛性。建立了在一般非凸问题（凸多面体锥上）度量次正则性成立的充分条件。数值实验表明，所提方法（尤其非单调自适应步长+GPU加速）在随机QCQP和核矩阵学习上表现优异。

Conclusion: 提出的rAPDB算法有效解决了非双线性耦合的凸非线性锥规划问题，在理论和实践上均表现出色，且度量次正则性的新结果具有独立理论价值。

Abstract: We propose restarted accelerated primal-dual algorithms with (non-monotone) backtracking (rAPDB) for convex nonlinear conic programs, with quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs) as a special case. Unlike linear and quadratic programs, these problems give rise to convex-concave minimax reformulations with non-bilinear coupling terms; therefore, the existing primal-dual methods for bilinear couplings are not applicable. To address this challenge, we build on the accelerated primal-dual method with adaptive stepsize search -- as it adapts to the local curvature -- and develop both fixed-frequency and adaptive restart schemes, incorporating both monotone and non-monotone adaptive step-size search strategies. The resulting algorithms require only first-order information and matrix-vector products, making them suitable for large-scale and GPU-accelerated implementation. Under metric subregularity of the KKT mapping, we prove a quadratic growth property for a self-centered smoothed duality gap and establish global linear convergence of the proposed restarted methods. We also establish sufficient conditions under which the metric subregularity holds even for general nonconvex problems over convex polyhedral cones. These results are new and may be of independent interest. Numerical experiments on random QCQPs and kernel matrix learning instances show that the proposed methods, especially with non-monotone adaptive stepsizes and GPU acceleration, achieve strong practical performance.

</details>


### [117] [Global optimization of quadratic root-difference minimization under elliptic annulus constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29294)
*Meijia Yang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆环约束下的非凸二次根差最小化问题(QR)，通过建立Annulus Brickman定理将其等价转化为二维凸问题(HP)，并利用Frank-Wolfe算法全局求解。关键发现是Frank-Wolfe子问题的解可直接作为原问题的O(1/√k)近似解，无需计算昂贵的二次系统恢复，据此提出高效的IMGE算法，数值实验证明其在大规模问题上显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆环约束下的非凸二次根差最小化问题在理论上具有挑战性，传统方法难以高效求解，尤其是大规模场景下计算开销巨大。

Method: 首先建立Annulus Brickman定理将非凸问题等价转化为二维凸问题(HP)；然后运用Frank-Wolfe算法全局求解(HP)；证明Frank-Wolfe子问题的解可直接作为原问题的O(1/√k)近似解，从而免去求解昂贵的二次系统恢复步骤；基于此恢复无关特性，提出迭代最小广义特征对(IMGE)算法。

Result: Frank-Wolfe子问题的解是原问题的O(1/√k)近似解；IMGE算法收敛迅速，在大规模问题上显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 通过将算法副产品转化为主要输出，完全绕过了计算昂贵的解恢复过程，为求解此类非凸问题提供了高效的新途径。

Abstract: This paper studies the nonconvex quadratic root-difference minimization under elliptic annulus constraints {\rm (QR)}. We first establish the Annulus Brickman theorem and equivalently reformulate {\rm (QR)} as a 2-dimensional convex problem {\rm (HP)} with hidden variables. We employ the Frank-Wolfe algorithm to globally solve {\rm (HP)}. A key finding is that the solutions of the Frank-Wolfe subproblems, which are traditionally viewed as mere auxiliary updates, are proven to be $O(1/\sqrt{k})$-approximate solutions of the original problem {\rm (QR)}. This transforms an algorithmic by-product into the primary output and completely bypasses the need to solve the computationally expensive quadratic system required for solution recovery. Leveraging this recovery-free property, we develop the efficient Iterative Minimum Generalized Eigenpair (IMGE) algorithm for globally solving {\rm (QR)}. Numerical experiments confirm that IMGE converges rapidly and significantly outperforms conventional methods, especially for large-scale problems.

</details>


### [118] [MoSSP: A Momentum-Based Single-Loop Stochastic Penalty Method for Nonconvex Constrained DC-Regularized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29635)
*Luxuan Li,Chunfeng Cui,Xiao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MoSSP算法，一种基于动量的单循环随机惩罚方法，用于解决带有DC正则化的非凸约束随机优化问题，实现了O(ε^{-4})和O(ε^{-3})的oracle复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单循环算法能高效求解无约束DC优化问题，但在约束优化场景下的潜力尚未充分探索。关键挑战在于在保持非凸约束可行性的同时获得良好的oracle复杂度。

Method: 将单随机近端梯度步应用于惩罚的Moreau包络加上凸DC部分，同时并行计算凹DC部分的近端映射。提出了两种变体：Polyak动量版本（O(ε^{-4})复杂度）和递归动量版本（O(ε^{-3})复杂度）。

Result: Polyak动量版本达到O(ε^{-4})的oracle复杂度，递归动量版本达到O(ε^{-3})的oracle复杂度，用于寻找随机ε-KKT点。实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: MoSSP成功弥合了单循环算法在无约束与约束DC优化之间的差距，为带有DC正则化的非凸约束随机优化问题提供了首个具有可证明复杂度保证的单循环方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a structured class of nonconvex constrained stochastic problems with difference-of-convex (DC) regularization, where the feasible set is possibly nonconvex and the concave part of the DC regularizer is allowed to be nonsmooth. The fundamental challenge lies in maintaining feasibility for nonconvex constraints while achieving favorable oracle complexity. Although single-loop algorithms efficiently solve unconstrained DC optimization problems, their potential for constrained optimization with DC structure remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we develop MoSSP, a Momentum-based Single-loop Stochastic Penalty method for such problems with provable complexity guarantees. The key idea is to apply a single stochastic proximal-gradient step to the Moreau envelope of the penalty plus the convex DC part, with the concave part's proximal mapping computed in parallel. We derive two algorithm variants: a Polyak-momentum version with $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ oracle complexity for finding stochastic $\varepsilon$-KKT points, and an improved $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ version incorporating recursive momentum. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [119] [Solving mathematical programs with complementarity constraints by disjunctive regularizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29757)
*Sebastian Lämmel,Vladimir Shikhman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对互补约束数学规划（MPCC）的析取正则化方法。该方法与Kanzow-Schwartz正则化具有相同的可行集，但函数描述不同。析取正则化在原始MPCC满足线性独立约束条件时，也能满足针对性的线性独立约束规格。它保持了Kanzow-Schwartz正则化的优良收敛性质，无需二阶必要条件即可收敛到MPCC的S-稳定点。此外，论文追踪了近似和极限非退化C-稳定点的拓扑类型（C-指标），证明了C-指标的二次和双活动部分在正则化下具有一般性的对应关系。数值实验表明，析取正则化明显优于Kanzow-Schwartz正则化，在高精度求解MPCC时甚至优于Scholtes正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPCC正则化方法（如Kanzow-Schwartz、Scholtes、符号型）在约束规格满足性、收敛性质或数值性能方面存在局限，需要一种既能保持良好理论性质又具有优越数值表现的新正则化方法。

Method: 提出析取正则化方法，利用逻辑OR运算符和等价的最大值型约束，构建与Kanzow-Schwartz正则化具有相同可行集但不同函数描述的新正则化形式。从理论上证明其约束规格性质、收敛性，并利用C-指标分析拓扑类型。通过数值实验对比Kanzow-Schwartz和Scholtes正则化。

Result: 析取正则化满足针对性的线性独立约束规格（若原MPCC满足）；无需二阶必要条件即可收敛至S-稳定点；C-指标的二次和双活动部分在正则化下具有一般对应关系；数值性能优于Kanzow-Schwartz正则化，高精度求解时甚至优于Scholtes正则化。

Conclusion: 析取正则化是一种兼具良好理论性质和优越数值性能的MPCC正则化方法，特别在高精度求解场景下具有显著优势，且揭示了C-指标在正则化过程中的新现象。

Abstract: We propose a new disjunctive regularization for mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCC). Its feasible set coincides with that of the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization. However, their functional descriptions differ considerably. For the disjunctive regularization, the logical operator OR and equivalent max-type constraints are used. Unlike the Kanzow-Schwartz, the disjunctive regularization satisfies the tailored linear independence constraint qualification if the original MPCC does. More than that, the favorable convergence properties - known to hold for the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization - remain valid for the disjunctive regularization as well. In particular, no second order necessary conditions are required to guarantee convergence towards S-stationary points of MPCC. Additionally, we keep track of the topological type of approximating and limiting nondegenerate C-stationary points in terms of their C-indices. Quadratic and biactive parts of the C-indices are shown to generically correspond to each other while regularizing. This is a new phenomenon as compared to the Scholtes or sign-type regularizations studied before. Numerical experiments illustrate that the proposed disjunctive regularization clearly outperforms the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization. Its numerical performance is even better than that of the Scholtes regularization if solving MPCCs with high accuracy.

</details>


### [120] [Mean Field Competition of Optimal Switching: The Vanishing Entropy Regularization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29892)
*Zongxia Liang,Shu Wang,Xiang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于排名的平均场博弈，其中竞争性智能体在多个努力水平之间进行策略性切换。通过引入熵正则化辅助问题，将切换决策随机化为连续时间有限状态马尔可夫链的转移概率控制，证明了正则化均衡的存在性、唯一性（在奖励方案凸性条件下），并可用虚拟博弈迭代逼近。当熵正则化消失时，正则化均衡收敛到原最优切换MFG中的松弛均衡，且在严格凸奖励方案下，松弛均衡下的总体排名分布具有唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于排名的平均场博弈中，智能体在多个努力水平间策略切换的均衡存在性、唯一性及计算方法问题，尤其是处理最优切换MFG中松弛均衡的理论性质。

Method: 提出熵正则化的辅助问题，将离散切换决策随机化为连续时间有限状态马尔可夫链的转移概率控制；利用凸性假设证明均衡唯一性；采用虚拟博弈迭代进行近似求解；通过正则化参数趋零分析建立收敛性。

Result: 证明了正则化均衡的存在性与唯一性（在凸奖励方案下）；虚拟博弈迭代可逼近该均衡；正则化均衡收敛到原问题的松弛均衡；严格凸奖励方案下松弛均衡的排名分布唯一。

Conclusion: 熵正则化方法为求解基于排名的MFG最优切换问题提供了一种有效的理论框架与计算途径，保证了均衡的适定性及可逼近性。

Abstract: This paper studies a type of rank-based mean field game in which competing agents strategically switch among multiple effort regimes. We propose an entropy regularized auxiliary problem where the switching decisions are randomized to the control of transition probability for a continuous-time finite-state Markov chain. We first establish the existence of regularized equilibrium in this auxiliary problem. Assuming the convexity of reward scheme, we then prove that the equilibrium is unique and can be approximated by a fictitious play iteration scheme. Furthermore, as the entropy regularization vanishes, we establish the convergence analysis of the regularized equilibrium towards the relaxed equilibrium in the original MFG of optimal switching. The uniqueness of the population ranking distribution under the relaxed equilibrium is also obtained given a strictly convex reward scheme.

</details>


### [121] [A New Constraint Qualification for Continuous-Time Nonlinear Programming Based on Asymptotic KKT Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29898)
*Rodrigo B. Moreira,Moisés R. C. do Monte,Valeriano A. de Oliveira*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为AKKT-正则性的新约束规格，该规格能够将满足渐近KKT条件的候选解集精炼到与经典KKT条件相同的精度。在AKKT-正则性下，每个局部最优解都满足KKT条件，且该约束规格是保证这一性质的最弱可能条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近KKT（AKKT）最优性条件虽然无需约束规格即可成立，但生成的候选解集不够鲁棒，某些满足AKKT条件的解甚至不是驻点。因此需要一种条件来精炼候选解集，使其达到经典KKT条件的精度。

Method: 引入一种名为AKKT-正则性的新约束规格，通过理论分析证明在该规格下每个局部最优解都满足KKT条件，并证明该规格是保证此性质的最弱条件，同时给出其适用性的充分条件。

Result: 在AKKT-正则性下，局部最优解满足KKT条件；该约束规格是保证此性质的最弱可能条件；给出了AKKT-正则性适用的充分条件。

Conclusion: AKKT-正则性成功地将AKKT条件的候选解集精炼至与经典KKT条件相同的精度，且作为约束规格具有最优性（最弱条件），为连续时间规划问题提供了实用的最优性条件框架。

Abstract: The asymptotic Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (AKKT) optimality conditions are distinguished from other approaches in the literature by virtue of their capacity to be effectively derived through numerical methods, such as the utilization of an appropriate version of the augmented Lagrange method. These tools are of a theoretical nature, yet they possess practical utility in the identification of candidate solutions to continuous-time programming problems. While this type of optimality condition is valid without imposing any constraint qualification, it is not sufficiently robust to generate good candidate solutions. In some cases, solutions satisfying AKKT conditions are not even stationary. In this study, we investigate conditions that effectively refine this set of candidate solutions with the same precision as the classical Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. This is achieved by introducing a novel constraint qualification, designated AKKT-regularity. It has been demonstrated that, under AKKT-regularity, each local optimal solution is shown to satisfy the KKT conditions. In addition, it is demonstrated that this constraint qualification is the weakest possible to ensure such a property. Furthermore, sufficient conditions are provided for its applicability.

</details>


### [122] [Accelerating Sinkhorn for Entropy-Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30267)
*Zeyi Xu,Long Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Acc-Sinkhorn，一种熵正则化最优传输的加速Sinkhorn变体，通过双层优化视角推导出保持Sinkhorn缩放形式的Nesterov加速方法，在可验证稳定性条件下达到O(1/k²)收敛率，实现Õ(n²/ε)复杂度，并在小正则化下获得10-30倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Sinkhorn算法在熵正则化最优传输中收敛速度较慢，尤其当正则化系数较小时需要大量迭代才能达到高精度，因此需要加速方法。

Method: 从双层优化视角出发：Sinkhorn行缩放精确求解内变量u，定义约简对偶目标f(v)=min_u F(u,v)；列缩放是v上的单步对偶镜像下降。基于此Hessian驱动结构引入Nesterov加速，保持Sinkhorn的缩放形式和每次迭代计算成本，仅使用Sinkhorn迭代的外推组合。

Result: 在可验证稳定性条件下达到O(1/k²)收敛率；非正则化OT的ε-近似复杂度为Õ(n²/ε)，相比Sinkhorn的Õ(n²/ε²)有显著改进；在合成问题、颜色迁移和词对齐任务中，小正则化下获得10-30倍加速。

Conclusion: Acc-Sinkhorn通过保持Sinkhorn简单缩放形式的同时引入Nesterov加速，显著提升了熵正则化最优传输的计算效率，为实际大规模OT应用提供了实用且高效的求解器。

Abstract: We propose Acc-Sinkhorn, a simple accelerated variant of Sinkhorn for entropy-regularized optimal transport (EOT). The method is derived from a bilevel optimization view: Sinkhorn row scaling solves the inner variable $u$ exactly and defines the reduced dual objective $f(v)=\min_u F(u,v)$, while the remaining column scaling is a unit-step dual mirror descent step in $v$. This structure yields a Hessian-driven Nesterov acceleration that keeps Sinkhorn's scaling form and per-iteration cost, using only extrapolated combinations of Sinkhorn iterates. We prove an $\mathcal{O}(1/k^2)$ rate under a verifiable stability condition. For an $\varepsilon$-approximation of unregularized OT, the resulting complexity is $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^2/\varepsilon)$, improved from $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^2/\varepsilon^2)$ for Sinkhorn. On synthetic problems, color transfer, and word alignment, Acc-Sinkhorn gives a $10\times$--$30\times$ speedup over Sinkhorn at small regularization.

</details>


### [123] [Proper efficiency results in vector optimisation in real linear-topological spaces based on vectorial penalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30286)
*Paul Schmölling,Christian Günther,Christiane Tammer,Elisabeth Köbis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实线性-拓扑空间中的约束向量优化问题，通过向量惩罚方法在锥凸性假设下探讨约束与无约束问题的真有效解集之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究约束向量优化问题与无约束向量优化问题的真有效解集之间的关系，利用向量惩罚方法在锥凸性假设下进行分析。

Method: 采用向量惩罚方法，在目标函数满足锥凸性假设的条件下，研究约束与无约束向量优化问题的真有效解集之间的关联。

Result: 通过向量惩罚方法，建立了约束与无约束向量优化问题真有效解集之间的联系。

Conclusion: 在锥凸性假设下，向量惩罚方法能够有效揭示约束与无约束向量优化问题真有效解集之间的关系。

Abstract: In this paper, we are dealing with constrained vector optimisation problems where the objective function acts between real linear-topological spaces. Our aim is to study the relationships between the sets of properly efficient solutions to constrained and unconstrained vector optimisation problems under certain cone convexity assumptions on the objective function using a vectorial penalisation approach.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于行为策略诱导度量的STHTD-MP时间差分方法，在原始-对偶鞍点公式中用对称Bellman矩阵替代协方差度量，通过Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤实现稳定离策略预测，理论分析和数值实验表明该方法在特定条件下比GTD2-MP具有更小的均值压缩因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度时间差分方法中，辅助变量度量的几何结构严重影响实际性能。Mirror-Prox TD方法通常使用特征协方差度量，而混合TD方法表明行为策略转移信息能提供更具信息量的更新几何结构。本文旨在探索如何利用行为诱导度量改进Mirror-Prox方法的性能。

Method: 提出STHTD-MP方法，在原始-对偶鞍点公式中将协方差度量替换为行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分；对混合鞍点算子应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤；保持原始变量和辅助变量使用单一学习率。理论分析采用ODE方法证明随机递归收敛，并基于确定性Mirror-Prox误差矩阵的谱半径导出投影oracle遍历间隙界和均值算子比较。

Result: 建立了在标准随机逼近假设下的形式化收敛分析（行为诱导度量正定、联合均值系统Hurwitz、有界性由Lyapunov论证保证）；推导了与GTD2-MP的精确均值算子比较；在Two-state、Random Walk和Boyan Chain基准上的数值均值算子分析支持理论条件；识别Baird反例为严格假设失效的奇异边界情况。

Conclusion: 当行为诱导度量改善鞍点几何结构时，STHTD-MP可以比GTD2-MP具有更小的均值压缩因子，从而在离策略线性预测中表现更优。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [125] [Behavior-Aware Auxiliary Corrections for Off-Policy Temporal-Difference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28855)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhiang He,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在线性预测设置下，将TDC/TDRC中的辅助协方差矩阵替换为行为贝尔曼矩阵，得到BA-TDC和BA-TDRC，分离了行为感知几何与正则化的贡献。通过有限状态均值系统分析、不动点保持和几乎必然收敛证明，实验表明行为感知替换在某些任务上有利，但正则化对复杂场景的鲁棒性能不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离策略TD学习在函数近似下的不稳定性问题，探究TDC中辅助协方差矩阵（C）替换为行为感知几何（行为贝尔曼矩阵A_μ）的效果，并分离行为感知几何与正则化各自对算法稳定性的贡献。

Method: 1) 将TDC的辅助矩阵C替换为行为贝尔曼矩阵A_μ，提出BA-TDC；2) 对BA-TDC进行正则化得到BA-TDRC；3) 给出有限状态均值系统公式化，证明不动点保持和几乎必然收敛（在实例化均值系统满足Hurwitz稳定性条件下）；4) 通过精确线性误差递归的谱半径比较确定性均值收敛速率。

Result: 在Two-State Counterexample、Baird's Counterexample、Random Walk和Boyan Chain实验上，行为感知替换在某些任务上本身非常有益，但在更困难的设置中，正则化对于鲁棒性能是必要的。

Conclusion: 行为感知几何替换能够提升某些场景下的TD学习性能，但正则化是确保算法在复杂任务中稳定鲁棒的关键因素，两者缺一不可。

Abstract: Temporal-difference learning with function approximation can be unstable under off-policy sampling. TDC stabilizes off-policy TD through an auxiliary covariance correction, and TDRC further regularizes this correction in a single-timescale recursion. This paper studies a behavior-aware replacement of the auxiliary covariance geometry in the linear prediction setting, which is the standard local model for understanding the feature-space dynamics of value-function approximation. We first replace the TDC auxiliary matrix (C) by the behavior Bellman matrix (A_μ), yielding BA-TDC, and then regularize the same behavior-aware equation to obtain BA-TDRC. This two-step construction separates the contribution of behavior-aware geometry from the contribution of regularization. The linear analysis also provides a tractable model for an auxiliary-geometry design question that arises in neural-network value approximation, where feature covariances and temporal transition matrices jointly shape the last-layer correction dynamics. We give a finite-state mean-system formulation, prove fixed-point preservation and almost-sure convergence under a Hurwitz stability condition on the instantiated mean system, and compare deterministic mean rates through the spectral radius of the exact linear error recursion. Experiments on the two-state counterexample, Baird's counterexample, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain show that the behavior-aware replacement can be highly beneficial by itself on some tasks, but that regularization is necessary for robust performance across harder settings.

</details>


### [126] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在科学论文评审中的应用，发现LLM评审与人工评审一致性有限，且存在作者利用LLM评审迭代修改论文以"刷分"的风险，最多可使35%的论文评分显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的论文评审正被越来越多地使用，甚至被大型会议官方试点。我们需要了解LLM评审的可靠性以及作者可能利用LLM修改论文的潜在影响。

Method: 基于2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文，从作者和审稿人两个视角进行实证实验：1）评估LLM评审与人工评审的一致性；2）测试不同提示词和模型的影响；3）模拟作者根据LLM评审迭代修改论文的"游戏"过程。

Result: LLM与人工评审对齐有限，最佳情况下也仅达到合理水平；不同提示词和模型下一致性差异显著；在特定场景下，作者通过迭代修改可有效"刷分"，最多35%的论文评分出现统计显著提升。

Conclusion: LLM评审存在被作者利用进行针对性修改的风险，需警惕其对学术评审公正性的潜在威胁。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [127] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法，通过将编辑型方法从加法参数更新重构为乘法正交变换，解决了概念擦除中精确性与生成能力保持之间的矛盾，在4.3秒内可擦除多达100个概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法存在局限：训练型方法计算成本高，编辑型方法虽高效但依赖加法参数更新，导致概念语义与生成能力在方向、幅度和角度几何上相互纠缠，难以同时实现精确擦除和生成能力保持。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE），将编辑型擦除从几何视角重构为乘法参数更新。通过闭式解推导层间正交变换，在保持神经元幅度和角度几何的同时实现精确概念擦除；针对多概念擦除冲突，引入子空间级目标与结构化子空间操作。

Result: 在单概念和多概念擦除任务上，OCE在概念擦除精度和非目标保持方面均优于现有方法，可在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。

Conclusion: OCE通过几何视角下的乘法正交变换，有效解耦了概念语义与生成能力，实现了高效、精确且可扩展的概念擦除。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [128] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VFEAgent，一个端到端多智能体系统，通过多模态视觉-语言管道和验证优先的代码合成框架，直接从输入图像和问题描述自动完成FEA建模与仿真，在多种工程力学场景下取得了优于LLM基线方法的成功率和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FEA工作流复杂且依赖领域专家知识，现有LLM集成方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限性，亟需一种能够自动从图像和文本描述生成完整FEA仿真的端到端解决方案。

Method: 提出VFEAgent系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于ReAct驱动的多模态视觉-语言多智能体管道，从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规范；(2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，集成自调试和回退机制，确保代码可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景下的系统评估表明，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面取得了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性上优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了自动化完整FEA工作流的可行性，该框架有潜力将工程师从繁琐的手动分析中解放出来。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [129] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议旨在通过建立以人为中心的建模与仿真基准，指导AI工具向负责任、合乎伦理的方向发展。该倡议利用开源基础设施和自动化测试评估AI工具在因果推理、模型构建、讨论等任务上的表现，发现不同LLM在各引擎上表现各异，没有单一模型全面占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界决策需要AI工具能够构建仿真模型并提供可解释的建议，但自动化建模工具必须补充而非取代人类专业知识。因此需要建立评估基准，确保AI建模工具的开发是负责任且合乎伦理的。

Method: BEAMS倡议通过开放的数字和组织基础设施，协作评估AI建模与仿真工具。设立指导组和技术组，分别负责优先排序和实现自动化测试基准。测试涵盖因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建议建模步骤和建议模型修复等类别，应用于定性/定量建模和模型讨论工具。

Result: 将sd ai项目引擎与不同LLM结合后，各工具表现存在差异。AI建模工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现优于因果推理和定量错误修复。没有单一LLM在所有引擎类型上占优，速度和准确率之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: BEAMS倡议已证明AI建模工具在不同任务类型上存在显著性能差异，未来将纳入解决偏见问题的基准，考虑替代视角和以人为中心的使用场景。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [130] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对约12,000名Bing Copilot用户和WildChat-4.8M数据的分析，发现用户与LLM的交互行为具有高度粘性，个体行为变化微弱，不同活跃度用户存在显著差异，且WildChat数据集偏向"超级用户"，不能代表典型用户交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对用户与LLM交互的描述多为静态，缺乏对个体用户行为随时间变化的了解，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 分析约12,000名随机抽样的Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行比较。

Result: 群体层面存在显著趋势，但个体用户轨迹趋势微弱；用户习惯高度粘性；高活跃用户对话更成功，任务更复杂和专业；WildChat数据集严重偏向高熟练度的"超级用户"。

Conclusion: 现有用户行为难以改变，用户异质性显著；WildChat不能代表典型用户-AI交互，这对该数据集的后续使用提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [131] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对联邦机构使用LLM对公众意见进行分类时，标准准确率评估无法检测不同模型对同一输入产生实质性不同分类的问题，提出了一种基于多模型分歧的解读审计流程，发现模型间主题分歧大于模型内提示变化，专家评分标准压制了深层分歧，以及人类标注者的修订常引入集体输出中不存在的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估仅依赖小规模验证集上的立场准确率，无法发现不同模型对同一公众意见产生不同分类的问题，这会影响政策制定者的决策。

Method: 提出Interpretive Audit Pipeline，利用多模型分歧作为解读复杂性的诊断信号，引导人工审查关注真正模糊的公众意见。对USDA的1,260条公众评论使用4个LLM进行分析，并在40条评论的二次标注研究中比较4个LLM与1位人类标注者的独立标注和修订行为。

Result: 模型间主题分歧大于模型内提示变化；专家评分标准压制了深层分歧但未解决分歧；人类标注者的修订常引入集体输出中不存在的框架。

Conclusion: 基于分歧的评估是LLM辅助解读编码中准确率指标的必要补充。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [132] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探究了提示语语气变化对LLM在客观选择题上准确率的影响，发现语气效应具有系统性但高度依赖模型，部分模型表现出显著的准确率波动，并提出了语气路由框架来解释内部推理模式的调谐机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的广泛使用，其性能被观察到因提示风格和语气而异，但缺乏系统性的量化研究。本文旨在揭示语气变化是否以及如何导致LLM在客观问题上的准确率差异。

Method: 使用两个数据集（50道基础题×5种语气变体，以及570道MMLU子集×7种语气变体），在ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite四个模型上进行实验，并提出了语气路由框架。

Result: 语气效应具有系统性但高度依赖模型：部分模型仅有微小但统计显著的偏移，另一些则表现出大幅准确率波动。同时发现了学科层面的语气敏感性差异。

Conclusion: 用户不应假设LLM在不同语气下具有稳健的可靠性，需警惕语气变化带来的性能不稳定问题。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [133] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一项针对高等教育从业者（n=72）的横断面调查，基于DOT框架（设计思维+开放系统理论），通过探索性因子分析识别出AI功能能力、监督治理、教师协作规划三维信念结构（α=.90），发现从业者认可AI教学支持但坚持人类监督，实践集中于迭代提示与内容生成，缺少需求评估与反馈循环，且面临政策、培训、基础设施等制度障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中AI整合的实践现状与理论框架之间存在认知缺口，缺乏基于实证的模型来描述从业者的信念、行为与制度条件之间的关系。

Method: 采用横断面调查（n=72），对19个信念条目进行探索性因子分析，基于DOT框架（设计思维+开放系统理论）进行理论建构。

Result: 识别出三维信念结构（AI功能能力、监督与治理、教师协作与规划，α=.90）；从业者认可AI但坚持人类监督；实践集中于迭代提示与内容生成；政策、培训、基础设施等制度障碍普遍存在。

Conclusion: DOT框架可作为描述性模型解释从业者信念与实践，但设计导向理论与当前实施之间存在差距；需进行验证性因子分析和结果导向研究。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [134] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D-BOS是一种一阶方法，通过将观察者的信念作为塑造目标并微分k步softmax-贝叶斯信念动态，实现多智能体强化学习中的对手塑造，在隐藏角色游戏中优于PPO和BBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手塑造方法通常在对手的参数、策略或价值空间内操作，而隐藏角色游戏中的信念操纵技术依赖硬编码目标（如欺骗或信念饱和），缺乏灵活性。需要一种能够自然从环境奖励结构中发现最优策略的信念级对手塑造方法。

Method: 提出可微信念级对手塑造(D-BOS)，将每个观察者的信念作为塑造目标，通过可微的k步softmax-贝叶斯信念动态进行端到端梯度反传；对多个观察者聚合各自信念轨迹上的梯度。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS在混合动机场景下提升最大，整体表现优于PPO和BBM。

Conclusion: 信念空间公式化为对手塑造提供了有效的梯度信号，允许欺骗或合作等策略从奖励结构中自然涌现，无需显式奖励设计。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [135] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构，结合连续记忆系统和语义相似性缓存，在多智能体流水线中实现端到端THS降低31.3%-35.9%，语义缓存命中率47.3%，在无须模型重训练的条件下同时提升事实可靠性、操作效率与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线中幻觉（hallucination）问题严重，不支持的声明可在各阶段不受约束地传播，缺乏有效的缓解机制与可观测性权衡的系统性研究。

Method: 采用HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构+连续记忆系统（CMS）+语义相似性缓存；构建混合基准（310个提示词，含217个认知不确定性提示和93个捏造诱导压力测试提示）；通过开放地板协议（OFP）编排三阶段智能体流水线；设计5个KPI（FCD/FGR/FDF/ECS/OSR）聚合为THS，配置5种权重方案研究缓解-可观测性权衡。

Result: 端到端THS降低31.3%-35.9%；语义缓存440次命中/930次潜在调用（47.3%命中率），LLM调用降至490次，降低能耗和CO2足迹；ExtremeObservability配置达到最负THS（-0.0709），证实高可观测性强化而非削弱缓解效果。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的多智能体设计可在无需模型重训练的情况下，同时改善事实可靠性、操作效率与可审计性，为生产级LLM系统提供可行的幻觉缓解方案。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [136] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COLAGUARD 是一种通过阶段式训练将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间的护栏模型，在匹配显式推理模型性能的同时，实现了12.9倍速度提升和22.4倍token使用减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的护栏模型虽然性能优于单纯分类方法，但存在高查询延迟和token开销，难以在实际高吞吐部署中使用。为此，需要一种兼顾安全鲁棒性和推理效率的替代方案。

Method: 提出COLAGUARD，通过阶段式训练课程（stage-wise training curriculum）将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间（continuous latent space），在推理时直接传播隐藏状态，避免了显式的逐token推理过程。

Result: 在10种提示/回复审核设置和8个安全基准上，COLAGUARD相比Llama Guard 3宏F1提升8.24分，与GuardReasoner（显式推理基线）宏F1相当，同时实现12.9倍加速和22.4倍token使用减少。

Conclusion: 潜在推理（latent reasoning）是显式推理生成的一种实用替代方案，能够在不牺牲安全鲁棒性的前提下大幅提升推理效率，证明两者并非相互竞争的目标。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [137] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种策略无关的执行与测量层，通过构建决策有效快照、定义标准化执行契约、记录多源分歧，将工业调度中的执行不确定性转化为可监督的结构化数据，实现对部署失配的可观测与可归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动调度策略在工业环境中面临决策状态时间不一致、动作可采纳性未明确定义、执行错误来源模糊等问题，限制了可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 提出一个策略无关的执行与测量中间层，包含三个核心功能：从异步事件流构建决策有效快照、定义含显式动作可采纳性的标准化执行契约、将执行结果记录为策略意图/事务结果/物理执行/人工干预之间的分歧。

Result: 通过离散事件仿真验证：在所有观测滞后 regimes 下均展现分析收益，将无差别执行失败转化为具有完整归因覆盖的结构化类型化结果；在低观测滞后场景下操作收益最强，可避免的执行错误能在提交前预防。

Conclusion: 该执行层将执行不确定性转化为可用于评估与策略优化的监督数据，实现了决策语义与执行行为之间的分离，使部署失配变得可观测且结构可归因。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation. The results show analytical benefits across all observation lag regimes, as undifferentiated execution failures are transformed into structured, typed outcomes with full attribution coverage. Operational benefits are strongest under low observation lag, where avoidable execution errors can be prevented before commitment. Overall, the layer turns execution uncertainty into supervisory data for evaluation and policy refinement.

</details>


### [138] [The Importance of Out-of-Band Metadata for Safe Autonomous Agents: The Redpanda Agentic Data Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29082)
*Tyler Akidau,Tyler Rockwood,Johannes Brüderl,Marc Millstone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP)架构，通过带外元数据通道在智能体的读写路径之外强制执行安全策略、数据范围限定和审计追踪，解决了AI智能体作为数字员工时因不可预测性和高能力带来的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越被期望作为数字员工运行，但它们既比人类更不可预测（易产生幻觉、误解和对抗性操纵），又比人类技术能力更强（具有深度系统知识和高速接口）。这种组合使得依赖智能体忠实地解释或传播安全关键元数据变得不安全。

Method: 提出Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP)架构，核心是带外元数据通道——在智能体的读写路径之外、跨异构基础设施确定性传输安全上下文、策略信号和审计追踪的基础设施路径。这些通道在智能体生命周期的每个阶段强制执行治理：输入时限定数据访问范围、执行时约束行为、输出时捕获防篡改记录。

Result: 通过一个多智能体投资组合再平衡系统进行演示，在该系统中自主智能体监控市场、做出交易决策并跨隔离客户账户执行订单，每个客户端的数据范围限定、交易批准阈值和防篡改审计追踪均由智能体既看不到也无法绕过的带外通道强制执行。

Conclusion: 带外元数据通道是一种有效的架构模式，可以在不依赖智能体自身可信度的情况下，强制执行安全策略、数据隔离和审计合规，从而安全地将AI智能体部署为数字员工。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly expected to operate as digital employees: accessing enterprise data, making decisions, and taking actions autonomously. But agents are simultaneously less predictable than humans -- prone to hallucination, misinterpretation, and adversarial manipulation -- and more technically capable: with deep system knowledge and high-throughput interfaces cascading damage at machine speed. This combination makes it unsafe to rely on agents to faithfully interpret or propagate security-critical metadata such as access policies, data classifications, and behavioral constraints.
  We present the Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP), an architecture built around out-of-band metadata channels: infrastructure pathways that carry security context, policy signals, and audit trails deterministically, entirely outside the agent's read and write path and across heterogeneous infrastructure. These channels enforce governance at every stage of the agent lifecycle -- scoping data access on the way in, constraining actions during execution, and capturing tamper-proof transcripts on the way out.
  We demonstrate ADP with a multi-agent portfolio rebalancing system in which autonomous agents monitor markets, make trade decisions, and execute orders across isolated client accounts -- with per-client data scoping, trade approval thresholds, and tamper-proof audit trails all enforced by out-of-band channels the agents can neither see nor bypass.

</details>


### [139] [Trends in AI and Human-AI Interaction in Clinical Trials -- A Hybrid Human-AI Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29096)
*Sandra Woolley,Tim Collins,Khalid Khattak,Illia Chernomorets,Ariane Arevalo,Chris Richardson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析ClinicalTrials.gov注册库中的记录，揭示了AI相关临床试验在时间上的增长趋势和地理分布特征，并探索了一种混合人机方法（GPT-5.5+人工审核）来筛选和分类AI临床试验记录。研究发现AI试验显著增加，中国和美国占比最高，且人机分类在识别非实质性AI研究时一致性较好，但在人机交互分类上存在分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解AI临床试验的时空分布趋势，并探索利用混合人机方法高效筛选和分类临床试验记录，以应对日益增长的AI相关试验数量带来的分析挑战。

Method: 从ClinicalTrials.gov检索AI相关临床试验记录，进行时间趋势和地理分布分析；采用混合人机工作流，使用GPT-5.5进行初步筛选和分类，再由人工审核验证，并以随机100条记录评估人机分类一致性。

Result: AI相关试验随时间显著增加，机器学习、深度学习、聊天机器人、GPT和大型语言模型相关引用增长迅速；中国和美国拥有最多AI相关试验，意大利、法国、西班牙、英国和土耳其近期增长显著；在100条随机样本中，人机分类在识别非实质性AI使用上一致性良好，但在人机交互分类（尤其是健康专业人员交互模糊时）一致性较低。

Conclusion: 混合人机筛选临床试验记录具有潜在可行性，但需要更清晰的试验报告和更精确的交互定义来提升分类效果。

Abstract: This paper examines records retrieved from the ClinicalTrials.gov registry to characterize temporal trends in AI terminology and the geographical distribution of AI trials. The work also reports on an exploratory hybrid human-AI approach to analyzing human-AI interaction trends in registered clinical trials. The hybrid workflow comprised a frontier generative AI model (GPT-5.5) and human review to screen and categorize records returned by an AI-focused search. The findings indicate a marked increase in AI-related trials over time, with recent growth in references to machine learning, deep learning, chatbots, GPTs, and large language models. Geographically, China and the United States accounted for the largest numbers of AI-related trials, with notable recent increases in several other countries including Italy, France, Spain, the UK and Turkey (Türkiye). In a random sample of 100 records, human and AI classifiers showed good agreement in identifying studies not substantively using AI, but lower agreement in classifying human-AI interaction, particularly where health professional interaction was ambiguous or insufficiently described. Overall, the results suggest that hybrid human-AI screening of clinical trial records is potentially viable, but clearer trial reporting and more precise interaction definitions will benefit the process.

</details>


### [140] [The Confidence Shortcut: A Reasoning Failure Mode of Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29123)
*Dueun Kim,Albert No*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了置信度对齐解码策略与复杂推理所需逻辑轨迹之间存在根本性不匹配，置信度对齐训练会放大这种偏差，导致高置信度错误，而传统的随机掩码反而能更好地保持推理轨迹条件，在复杂推理任务中表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前掩码扩散语言模型（MDMs）采用置信度基解码作为默认推理策略，且近期训练方案试图将训练掩码模式与生成时观察到的模式对齐。但作者认为这种置信度对齐与复杂推理所需的逻辑流轨迹存在根本性不匹配，有必要揭示并解决这一问题。

Method: 通过多位数加法等推理任务进行实证分析，对比置信度基解码与随机掩码策略在不同推理复杂度下的表现，特别关注长程依赖关系下的错误模式。

Result: 置信度对齐训练在复杂推理输入上将错误率放大了一个数量级；在五个推理任务中，置信度基解码在高度复杂输入上诱导失败，置信度对齐训练加剧了这些问题；而随机掩码稳健地保持了推理轨迹条件。

Conclusion: 置信度对齐解码与复杂推理的内在逻辑不匹配，应该重新审视MDMs的推理策略，随机掩码尽管看似效率较低，但在处理复杂推理尾部数据时更为稳健。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) uniquely support any-order generation, with confidence-based decoding currently serving as the de facto standard inference policy. To optimize for this, recent training schemes attempt to align training mask patterns directly with those observed during generation. However, we argue that confidence-based decoding is inherently misaligned with the logical-flow trajectories required for complex reasoning, and that confidence-aligned training actively entrenches this misalignment. We make this concrete using multi-digit addition, where the decoding strategy prematurely predicts locally easy digits before resolving their long-range dependencies, producing high-confidence errors on challenging inputs. While traditional random masking keeps the failure rate low on this challenging tail, confidence-aligned training amplifies the error rate by an order of magnitude. Across five distinct reasoning tasks, this same pattern emerges with task-dependent severity: confidence-based decoding induces failures on highly complex inputs, and confidence-aligned training exacerbates them. In contrast, random masking -- despite its perceived inefficiency -- robustly preserves the reasoning-trajectory conditionals essential for solving the challenging tail.

</details>


### [141] [Governing Technical Debt in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29129)
*Muhammad Zia Hydari,Raja Iqbal,Narayan Ramasubbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了**Agentic技术债务**（因提示、记忆、工具模式、编排图、控制策略和可观测性例程被快速拼凑而累积的设计与治理负债）和**随机税**（因概率性智能体行为超出可接受边界而产生的持续运营成本），并提出了通过轻量级仪表盘和治理控制使两者可视化的管理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件和预测性ML的技术债务框架无法完全捕捉智能体AI系统在生产基础设施中产生的治理挑战，因此需要新的概念来定义和区分这类系统特有的累积负债与持续运营成本。

Method: 通过概念定义与区分（债务为存量、税为流量），并建议采用轻量级仪表盘和治理控制手段来使这两类成本对管理者可见。

Result: 提出了Agentic技术债务和随机税两个核心概念，阐明了二者的本质区别（债务是设计/治理负债的存量，税是运营成本的流量），并给出了管理建议。

Conclusion: 智能体AI系统的治理需要区分两类成本：积累性的技术债务和持续性的随机税，管理者可通过轻量级工具实现这两者的可视化与管控。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly being explored as production infrastructure: they reason over multiple steps, call tools, act through workflows, and adapt through memory and feedback. These systems create governance challenges that are not fully captured by traditional software or predictive ML technical debt. We define Agentic Technical Debt as the accumulated liability created when prompts, memory, tool schemas, orchestration graphs, control policies, and observability routines are patched together faster than they can be validated, standardized, and governed. We define Stochastic Tax as the recurring operating burden of keeping probabilistic agent behavior within acceptable bounds. The distinction matters: debt is a stock of design and governance liability, while the tax is a flow of operating cost that arises because stochastic agents act through tools and workflows. We outline how managers can make both visible through lightweight dashboards and governance controls.

</details>


### [142] [Better Later Than Sooner: Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge Graph Construction via Ontology-grounded Post-extraction Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29168)
*Lorenzo Loconte,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种本体引导的神经符号知识图谱构建框架，通过后提取阶段的LLM修正来提升KG一致性，同时保持下游QA质量并减少token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的图RAG方法缺乏支持符号操作（如聚合、穷举）的结构，而符号化图方法使用LLM提取KG时存在一致性问题，提取的事实可能违反常识本体约束。

Method: 神经符号框架：开放域提取 + 嵌入驱动的类型和谓词规范化 + 针对性的LLM本体违例修正。将修正推迟到后提取阶段，避免重复LLM调用。

Result: 减少了token使用，提升了KG一致性，并保持了下游QA质量；通过SPARQL图模式出现频率验证了提取的KG适合符号化查询。

Conclusion: 后提取阶段的LLM修正策略在效率和一致性之间取得了良好平衡，生成的KG支持SQL式符号查询，有助于复杂QA任务。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) is a core challenge in AI, particularly for complex queries requiring multi-hop reasoning across documents, or symbolic operations like aggregation or exhaustive listing. Retrieval-augmented generation has become the dominant approach to QA, with recent graph-based variants addressing part of these issues by organizing knowledge to better support compositional questions. However, most textual graph-based RAG methods still lack the structure needed for symbolic operations useful to answer complex questions reliably. This motivates symbolic graph-based approaches, which extract knowledge graphs (KGs) whose relations are logic predicates that enable SQL-like querying. Yet these pipelines typically use LLMs for KG extraction, which can introduce consistency issues, where extracted facts may violate commonsense ontology constraints. We propose a neuro-symbolic framework for ontology-grounded KG construction combining open-domain extraction, embedding-based canonicalization of types and predicates, and targeted LLM-based correction of ontology violations. By deferring corrections to a post-extraction stage, our method avoids repeated LLM calls, substantially reducing token usage while improving KG consistency and preserving downstream QA quality. Finally, we show that the extracted KGs are well suited for symbolic querying by measuring the occurrence of SPARQL graph patterns.

</details>


### [143] [ReasonOps: Operator Segmentation for LLM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29192)
*Daniel Lee,Owen Queen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonOps，一种无监督、高表达力的方法，用于为大型推理模型的思维链轨迹标注简洁的通用算子。通过对44,662条来自12种思考型LLM的轨迹进行分析，发现所有模型共享7种重复出现的推理算子（如回溯、推断、假设），这些算子在不同模型家族和基准域中普遍存在。算子序列具有模型特异性，可高精度识别来源模型，并能有效预测答案正确性，甚至在轨迹完成前即可进行早期质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型的思维链轨迹可长达数万token，但缺乏描述其内部结构的词汇。现有方法要么过于僵化，要么表达力不足，无法跨领域和跨模型捕捉特征。

Method: ReasonOps采用无监督聚类方法，以句子开头的3-token枢轴为特征，从44,662条来自12种LLM（涵盖6个家族、8个推理基准）的轨迹中自动发现7种通用推理算子（回溯、推断、假设等语篇层级操作），并通过三位独立LLM评判员对留出样本进行70-76%准确率的验证。

Result: 发现7种跨模型家族和基准域的通用推理算子；算子分布可高精度识别来源模型（macro-AUC）；结构化的算子特征在WP-AUC上预测答案正确性显著优于基线；在轨迹完成50%时即可达到WP-AUC的早期质量预测。

Conclusion: ReasonOps提供了一种无监督、无需标注的通用分析框架，能够深入洞察LLM推理轨迹的内部结构，在模型识别、正确性预测和早期质量评估等下游任务上均取得强有力结果。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought traces from large reasoning models can span tens of thousands of tokens, yet we lack a vocabulary for describing their internal structure. Previous methods developed to analyze chain-of-thought traces are either too rigid or not expressive enough, failing to capture features across domains and models. To remedy this, we develop ReasonOps, an unsupervised, expressive method for annotating chain-of-thought traces, providing succinct universal operators. Using ReasonOps, we analyze 44,662 traces from 12 thinking LLMs spanning 6 families across 8 reasoning benchmarks and discover that they share a common compositional structure: 7 recurring reasoning operators -- discourse-level moves such as backtracking, inferring, and hypothesizing -- that emerge from unsupervised clustering of sentence-initial 3-token pivots. These operators appear across every model family and benchmark domain, confirmed by three independent LLM judges who classify held-out samples at 70 -76% accuracy. We analyze the structure of operators on easy vs. hard problems, revealing that reflective operators are more helpful on hard problems and harm performance on easy problems. Operator sequences are highly model-identifying: a classifier trained on operator distributions alone recovers the source model with macro-AUC, revealing that each model family has a distinctive reasoning fingerprint. Structural operator features predict within-problem answer correctness well above baselines. Classifiers built on these operators reach WP-AUC and on AIME specifically. ReasonOps further enables early quality estimation well before the trace completes: we predict at WP-AUC for only 50% of the trace. The ReasonOps pipeline is unsupervised and annotation-free, enabling deep insights into LLM reasoning traces as well as strong downstream results on model identification and correctness prediction.

</details>


### [144] [BenchTrace: A Benchmark for Testing Reflection Ability and Controlled Evolution in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29225)
*Jiahao Huang,Fei Cheng,Junfeng Jiang,Zefan Yu,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BenchTrace基准，用于评估LLM智能体的自我进化能力，包含反思评估和进化评估两部分，实验发现当前模型在自我进化上存在诊断瓶颈、早期遗忘和负迁移等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化智能体的评估存在两个局限：仅衡量任务得分而忽略反思质量，且依赖智能体自身的回合运行，无法针对特定失败模式进行测试。

Method: 构建1,821条带注释的片段-反思数据集，涵盖六种任务；提出反思评估（通过目标QA任务探测失败识别能力）和进化评估（在受控自我进化模拟中测试失败经验是否转化为规避行为）；提出新的评估指标FAR（失败规避率）。

Result: Qwen3-32B和GPT-4.1在反思评估端到端通过率均低于30%，诊断是主要瓶颈；自我进化方法普遍提升FAR，但随噪声片段累积出现早期遗忘，且反思无法泛化到其他任务上下文导致负迁移；仅完全正确的反思与高FAR强相关。

Conclusion: BenchTrace揭示了当前自我进化方法的具体局限，并提供了一个可控、模型无关的针对性评估框架。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve over time by reflecting on past failures, but existing evaluation is limited in two ways: it measures only task scores, leaving reflection quality unknown, and it relies on agents' own episode runs, offering no mechanism to target specific failure patterns. We present \textbf{BenchTrace}, a benchmark for evaluating self-evolution ability in LLM agents. BenchTrace is built on a snapshot-reflection dataset of 1,821 annotated episodes spanning six diverse tasks, and comprises a \textbf{Reflection Evaluation} that probes failure identification through targeted QA tasks, and an \textbf{Evolution Evaluation} that tests whether past failure experience translates into avoidance behavior in a controlled self-evolution simulation. Building on BenchTrace, we propose \textbf{failure avoidance rate (FAR)}, a new evaluation metric measuring the fraction of test cases in which the agent successfully avoids the target failure instance. Experiments with Qwen3-32B and GPT-4.1 reveal that both models fall below a 30\% end-to-end pass rate on reflection evaluation, with diagnosis as the primary bottleneck. Evolution evaluation shows that self-evolution methods generally improve FAR over the non-evolving baseline, but agents forget early lessons as noise episodes accumulate, and agents fail to generalize their reflections beyond the specific context, causing negative transfer across task contexts. Our correlation analysis further reveals that only a fully correct reflection is strongly associated with higher FAR. BenchTrace exposes concrete limits of current self-evolution approaches and provides a controlled, model-agnostic framework for targeted evaluation.

</details>


### [145] [Tailoring the Curriculum: Student-Centered Reasoning Distillation via Dynamic Data-Model Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29229)
*Jiahao Huang,Fei Cheng,Junfeng Jiang,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标，用于评估数据集与小型学生模型在推理蒸馏中的适配度，通过联合考虑数据质量、相对难度和学生能力，验证了DMC与推理蒸馏性能的强相关性，并证明基于DMC进行数据选择（包括动态选择）能显著提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理蒸馏将大语言模型的复杂推理能力迁移至小模型，但训练数据与学生模型的匹配程度直接影响蒸馏效果，现有方法缺乏有效评估这种匹配度的指标。

Method: 提出DMC指标，联合建模数据质量、相对难度和学生能力三个维度；从相关性分析和数据选择两个角度验证；并引入训练过程中基于DMC的动态数据选择策略。

Result: DMC与推理蒸馏性能呈现强相关性；基于DMC进行数据选择可提升蒸馏性能；动态数据选择进一步优于静态选择，效果在多个学生模型和任务上保持一致。

Conclusion: DMC是一个有效的数据-模型兼容性评估指标，能指导数据选择以优化推理蒸馏效果，且动态选择策略具有更优潜力。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation transfers complex reasoning abilities from large language models (LLMs) to smaller ones, yet its success depends on how well the training data align with the student model. This paper introduces the Data-Model Compatibility (DMC) metric, which can be used to assess the suitability of a dataset for reasoning distillation on a student model. DMC provides an assessment by jointly considering data quality, relative difficulty, and student capability. We validated the effectiveness of DMC from two perspectives: (1) DMC exhibits a strong correlation with reasoning distillation performance; and (2) using DMC as the criterion for data selection leads to improved reasoning distillation performance. Both findings are consistently demonstrated across multiple student models and tasks. Moreover, since the DMC of each dataset dynamically changes during training, our experiments demonstrate that dynamically selecting datasets based on DMC can further enhance performance.

</details>


### [146] [Rethinking Literature Search Evaluation: Deep Research Helps, and Human Citation Lists Are Not a Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29234)
*Gaurav Sahu,Laurent Charlin,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从两个角度研究大规模文献检索：改进检索流程（Deep Research流水线将召回率从20%提升至80%+），以及质疑人工参考文献作为评估基准的可靠性（仅51%的人为引用被评为中等相关，且人类引用直接合作者的概率是AI的2.5倍）。结论主张多维度联合评估文献检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献检索评估依赖人工参考文献列表作为黄金标准，但其可靠性未经严格检验；同时，传统API检索在全面性上存在显著不足。本文旨在同时改进检索方法并拷问评估基准的有效性。

Method: 1) 构建Deep Research流水线：处理完整查询论文，沿其参考文献进行广度优先扩展检索；2) 使用中性LLM作为裁判，评估人工引用与AI重排序结果的相关性；3) 在OpenAlex合著图上分析引用偏差（合作者引用概率）。

Result: Deep Research流水线将RollingEval-Jun25基准上的召回率从低于20%提升至80%以上。仅51%的人工引用被评为中等相关及以上，而最强AI重排序器达86-88%。人类引用直接合作者的概率比最佳AI重排序器高2.5倍。

Conclusion: 不应采用单一维度的文献检索评估，而应联合报告召回率、主题相关性评分、排序列表多样性和合著距离诊断等互补指标。

Abstract: We study large-scale literature search from two complementary angles: improving the retrieval pipeline, and stress-testing the human reference list as an evaluation target. First, we implement a Deep Research pipeline that processes the full query paper and expands the retrieved results breadth-first along their bibliographies, and show that it substantially outperforms vanilla API-only search, raising recall on RollingEval-Jun25 (a 250-paper literature-search benchmark) from below 20% to above 80%. Second, we use a neutral LLM-as-a-judge to determine if human references are sound ground truth for the task. We find significant limitations: only 51% of human citations are judged moderately relevant or higher, against 86--88% for the strongest AI-based re-rankers. We study this gap on the OpenAlex co-authorship graph, finding that humans are 2.5x more likely than the best AI re-rankers to cite a direct collaborator. Together, our results argue against single-axis literature-search evaluation: recall, topical-relevance scoring, ranked-list diversity, and a co-authorship-distance diagnostic each measure complementary properties of citation quality and should be reported jointly.

</details>


### [147] [Surfacing Isolated Learners with Outcome-Independent Mediation of Feedback between Teachers and Students Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29240)
*Junsoo Park,Youssef Medhat,Htet Phyo Wai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需成绩或事后标签的可解释决策层，通过整合学生学习困难普遍性、自我报告与观察困难的不一致性、以及未解决的教师关注三个信号，在AI增强课堂中生成需要关注的主题优先级排名，并在研究生CS课程中验证了其与教师关注和学生困难的高相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI增强课堂产生丰富的师生反馈，但这些信号难以转化为及时的教学决策；现有方法依赖成绩或事后标签，缺乏透明性和可解释性。

Method: 提出一种可解释决策层，整合三个信号：学生学习困难普遍性（gap prevalence）、学习者自我报告与观察困难之间的不一致性（disagreement）以及未解决的教师关注（unresolved teacher concerns）。输出为带每个主题决策记录的有序主题优先级列表。

Result: 在一门研究生CS课程中（5次教师访谈，279份调查问卷），优先级主题与教师关注相关性高（top-5重叠3/5，Spearman ρ=0.80），与学生报告的主题困难相关（ρ=0.46，p=.048）。多信号整合能识别单一信号无法发现的学习者（AUC=0.96 vs 0.91）。反思性思维、求助行为、自我效能等行为信号与学习相关构念一致。

Conclusion: 透明协调机制可能在反馈不完整时支持人类-AI协同决策，尽管结果尚属初步，但表明该方法具备潜力。

Abstract: AI-augmented classrooms generate rich teacher and student feedback before graded outcomes become available, yet these signals can be difficult to translate into timely instructional decisions. We propose an interpretable decision layer: a transparent mechanism that ranks course topics requiring attention without using grades or post-hoc outcome labels. The approach combines three signals: student learning difficulty prevalence, disagreement between learner self-reports and observed difficulties, and unresolved teacher concerns. The output is a ranked set of topic priorities with per-topic decision records explaining each ranking. In one graduate CS course offering ($n=5$ instructor interviews; $n=279$ survey responses), prioritized topics aligned with instructor concerns (top-5 overlap 3/5; Spearman $ρ=0.80$) and student-reported topic difficulty ($ρ=0.46$, $p=.048$). Multi-signal integration also surfaced learners not identified through individual signal sources alone (AUC $=0.96$ vs. $0.91$ for gap prevalence alone). Reflective thinking, help-seeking, and self-efficacy provided additional evidence that student behavioral signals align with learning-related constructs. While preliminary, these findings suggest that transparent coordination mechanisms may help support human-AI co-agency when feedback is incomplete.

</details>


### [148] [DenseSteer: Steering Small Language Models towards Dense Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29247)
*Yang Ouyang,Shuhang Lin,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DenseSteer框架，通过将小型语言模型的内部表征向"密集推理"模式调整，在不增加token级负对数似然的情况下提升其数学推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型具备强链式推理能力，而小模型（≤3B参数）在多步推理任务上显著落后。作者希望找到一种无需额外训练的方法来提升小模型的推理表现。

Method: 通过分析Qwen-2.5模型族在数学推理基准上的表现，发现推理能力强的模型具有更少推理步骤但每步信息密度更高的"密集推理"特性。基于此，提出DenseSteer——一种无需训练、仅在推理时通过调节内部表征朝向密集推理模式来增强小模型推理的框架。

Result: DenseSteer在小模型上取得一致的准确率提升，且不增加token级的负对数似然。

Conclusion: 密集推理是数学问题求解中一种有效的结构化方法，为提升小模型推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning abilities, while smaller models (<= 3B parameters) significantly underperform on multi-step reasoning tasks. Based on empirical analyses of the Qwen-2.5 model family on math reasoning benchmarks, we find that more proficient reasoning is associated with fewer reasoning steps but higher information density per step, a property we term Dense Reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we propose DenseSteer, a training-free inference-time steering framework that enhances small-model reasoning by modulating internal representations toward dense reasoning patterns. Experiments show that our method yields consistent accuracy improvements without increasing token-level Negative Log-Likelihood, highlighting dense reasoning as an effective structural approach to mathematical problem solving.

</details>


### [149] [Provably Secure Agent Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29251)
*Benlong Wu,Weiming Zhang,Kejiang Chen,Han Fang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于逻辑推理根本限制的智能体安全新范式，通过可执行证明约束动作（ePCA）框架，采用神经符号隔离架构，将智能体意图无损形式化为一阶逻辑数学约束，在宏观和微观二维动态对抗系统中实现了零攻击成功率和零误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型从受限生成引擎向具有广泛执行权限的智能体转变时，AI失控引发人工智能安全根本危机。现有依赖经验语义护栏和概率性大模型裁决者的防御架构在面对复杂语义符号解耦攻击时无法提供确定性安全下限，亟需克服经验语义护栏困境。

Method: 提出基于逻辑推理根本限制的安全新范式，并引入可执行证明约束动作（ePCA）框架。该框架采用神经符号隔离架构，放弃对自然语言的语义信任，在智能体执行物理操作前强制将其意图无损形式化为一阶逻辑数学约束，通过形式化验证机制确保安全性。

Result: 在宏观和微观二维动态对抗系统的实证评估中，形式化验证机制在评估场景下实现了零攻击成功率和零误报率，且计算延迟极低。

Conclusion: 该研究在显式系统假设下提供了有条件的形式化基础，为构建未来智能系统的底层防御基础提供了工程范式。

Abstract: As large language models transition from bounded generative engines to agents with expansive execution privileges, AI going out of control precipitates a fundamental crisis in artificial intelligence security. Existing defense architectures heavily rely on empirical semantic guardrails and probabilistic large model adjudicators, mechanisms that fail to provide deterministic security lower bounds when facing complex semantic symbol decoupling attacks. To overcome this empirical semantic guardrail dilemma, this paper proposes a new security paradigm for agents based on the fundamental limitations of logical reasoning. Based on this paradigm, we further introduce an executable Proof-Constrained Action (ePCA) framework with a neural symbolic isolation architecture. This framework abandons semantic trust in natural language, forcing agents to losslessly formalize their intentions into first-order logical mathematical constraints before performing physical operations. Empirical evaluations of macroscopic and microscopic two-dimensional dynamic adversarial systems demonstrate that our formal verification mechanism achieves zero attack success rate and zero false positive rate across the evaluated scenarios, with extremely low computational latency. This research provides a conditional formal foundation under explicit system assumptions and an engineering paradigm for constructing the underlying defense foundation for future intelligent systems.

</details>


### [150] [OpenClawBench: Benchmarking Process-side Anomalies in Real-world Agent Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29253)
*Yibing Liu,Yangze Liu,Xiaolong Yin,Bin Wang,Chong Zhang,Hao Yin,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenClawBench数据集和FullTax标注框架，揭示智能体执行中任务成功但过程存在异常的"结果-过程鸿沟"，并提供可审计、可复用的监督数据来诊断和监控运行时智能体可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注任务结果是否成功，忽视了执行过程中可能存在的未消歧义、不安全外部写入、被忽略错误等过程侧异常，导致成功导向评估掩盖真实的过程失败。

Method: 基于BFCL驱动的OpenClaw会话构建包含31,264条带标注轨迹的大规模数据集；提出FullTax框架将轨迹转换为结构化异常监督，包括二元标签、支持证据、起止定位、严重性、可恢复性及5类异常分类体系。

Result: 在31,135条通过oracle的成功执行中，仍有2,904条被FullTax标记为过程异常；基于LoRA微调的Gemma 3 12B检测器在干净标签测试集上达到二元F1=0.729。

Conclusion: 仅以结果成功评估智能体会遗漏重要的过程侧失败，OpenClawBench和FullTax将真实执行日志转化为可审计、可复用的监督资源，用于研究、诊断和监控运行时智能体可靠性。

Abstract: Task success can hide process anomalies in real-world agent executions. An agent may pass the final task oracle while still accumulating unresolved ambiguity, unsafe external writes, ignored errors, weakly grounded commitments, or capability-boundary overcommitment.
