Method: 纵向研究设计，测试通用AI工具（Gemini）和教育专用AI工具（Coteach）使用Task Analysis Guide (TAG)分类数学任务认知需求的能力。分别在基线、模型版本更新后以及使用少样本提示（每个认知需求类别两个示例任务）后进行测试。

Result: Gemini在版本更新后准确率稳定在58%；Coteach准确率从75%下降至50%。少样本提示后，Gemini提升至67%，Coteach恢复至75%。

Conclusion: 提示工程技术比被动模型更新对AI工具在专业教育任务上的性能提升有更大且更可靠的效果；模型版本更新并不总能带来性能提升。研究对教育情境中AI工具的选择、评估和实施具有重要启示。

Abstract: As AI tools become increasingly integrated into educational contexts, questions arise about both their stability over time and their responsiveness to prompt engineering techniques. This longitudinal study focused on different AI tools' ability to use the Task Analysis Guide (TAG; Stein \& Smith, 1998) to classify the cognitive demand of mathematics tasks. In particular, it examined whether this classification ability changed with (1) model version updates over time and (2) few-shot prompting using exemplar tasks. We tested a general-purpose AI tool (Gemini) and an education-specific AI tool (Coteach). The specific tools were selected because of their relatively high performance on relevant published benchmarks and prior task-specific tests. Models were tested at baseline, retested with model version updates, and then tested again using few-shot prompting (two exemplar tasks for each cognitive demand category). Results revealed that newer model versions alone produced mixed effects: Gemini's accuracy remained stable at 58\%, while Coteach's accuracy decreased from 75\% to 50\%. However, few-shot prompting improved both models' performance: Gemini increased to 67\% and Coteach recovered to 75\% accuracy. These findings demonstrate that prompt engineering techniques can have larger and more reliable effects than passive model improvements, and that version updates may not always improve performance on specialized educational tasks. The study has important implications for how educators and researchers should approach AI tool selection, evaluation, and implementation in educational contexts.

</details>


### [224] [BioRefusalAudit: Auditing Biosecurity Refusal Depth Using General and Domain-Fine-Tuned Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30162)
*Caleb DeLeeuw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了语言模型在生物安全场景下拒绝行为的结构性鲁棒性，发现所有模型均无法干净区分良性内容与生物危害。拒绝行为会因提示框架、格式或输出长度限制而消失；模型存在过度拒绝或从不拒绝等问题。本文还提出了一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的散度分数D，用于内部激活层面检测行为评估难以发现的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物安全评估仅关注模型是否产生有害输出，忽略了拒绝行为的结构稳定性问题。本文旨在回答：当模型拒绝时，这种拒绝是否在提示框架、格式或输出长度等变化下依然稳健？同时，探索内部激活层面的审计是否能揭示行为评估无法发现的缺陷。

Method: 跨5种架构（Gemma 2 2B-IT、Gemma 4 E2B-IT、Qwen 2.5 1.5B、Phi-3-mini、Llama 3.2 1B）进行提示框架、格式和输出长度变化测试；引入散度分数D，比较模型表层响应标签与内部SAE特征激活差异；在Gemma 2 2B-IT和Gemma 4 E2B-IT上计算完整D值，并在消费者硬件（GTX 1650 Ti Max-Q + Colab T4）上完成实验。

Result: 所有模型均未能干净区分良性内容与生物危害。Gemma 2 2B-IT从不真正拒绝；Gemma 4 E2B-IT在聊天模板格式下拒绝65/75，无格式时0/75；两者在80 token限制下拒绝率降至0%。Qwen 2.5 1.5B和Phi-3-mini过度拒绝（83-87%良性内容被标记为有害）。Llama 3.2 1B表现出唯一的有效梯度（61点跨度）。模型对合法但文化敏感物质（如裸盖菇素）的拒绝率甚至超过真实生物危害。Gemma 4上，comply与refuse响应D值差距0.647点，零重叠（n=75）。

Conclusion: 行为评估不足以发现模型安全机制的结构性缺陷；激活层面的审计（如散度分数D）能够暴露行为评估无法捕捉的失败模式，但该工作仍属初步，存在目录窄、样本内校准、仅覆盖Gemma系列SAE等局限。

Abstract: Biosecurity evaluations of language models typically ask whether models produce hazardous output. This paper asks a complementary question: when a model refuses, is that refusal structurally sound, or does it disappear under modest changes to prompt framing, formatting, or output length? Across five architectures, no model cleanly discriminated benign from hazard. Gemma 2 2B-IT never genuinely refused across 75 prompts, hedging on every hazard-adjacent query. Gemma 4 E2B-IT refused 65/75 prompts with chat-template formatting and 0/75 without it. Both Gemma models collapsed to 0% under an 80-token cap. Qwen 2.5 1.5B and Phi-3-mini over-refused, flagging 83-87% of benign biology as hazardous. Llama 3.2 1B showed the only meaningful tier gradient (61-point spread). To probe what drives such over-refusal, we tested a panel of Schedule I but biologically non-toxic compounds (notably psilocybin cultivation, with FDA Breakthrough Therapy status). Some models refused these at rates exceeding genuinely hazardous biology, suggesting refusal tracks legality and cultural salience over CBRN hazard. To measure the internal side, we introduce a divergence score D comparing a model's surface response label to its internal sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature activations. Full D was computed on Gemma 2 2B-IT (Gemma Scope 1) and Gemma 4 E2B-IT (author-trained bio SAE). Two fine-tuned Gemma 2 domain SAEs were released. On Gemma 4, comply and refuse responses separated by a 0.647-point gap with zero overlap (n=75), though this is preliminary, with a narrow catalog, within-sample calibration, and Gemma-family-only SAE coverage. Built over one hackathon weekend on consumer hardware (GTX 1650 Ti Max-Q, plus Colab T4 for SAE training), this preliminary evidence suggests activation-level auditing may surface failure modes invisible to behavioral evaluation, with substantial variation across architectures.

</details>


### [225] [Modularizing Educational LLM-Agency for Fostering Responsible Learning Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30187)
*Julius Gabelmann,Felix Jahn,Kevin Baum,Sophie van Rossum,Emely Wuenscher,Timo P. Gros,Verena Wolf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化的智能体AI聊天机器人架构，用于辅导学生解题，旨在通过增强可控性、透明性和可监督性，促进AI在教育中的负责任使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人在教育中的广泛采用会深刻改变学习方式，但现有LLM缺乏遵循教学法概念的倾向，可能导致学生迁移能力、批判性思维和创造力的损失，因此需要更负责任的AI教育部署方案。

Method: 首先识别负责任的基于LLM的教育系统应满足的若干期望标准，论证单体式开箱即用解决方案的结构性缺陷，然后提出模块化的智能体架构，为解题的不同阶段设计特定模块，以融入有针对性的教学建议。

Result: 提出了一种模块化智能体AI聊天机器人架构，能够以更可控、透明和可监督的方式引导学生完成学习过程。

Conclusion: 模块化智能体架构通过将教学建议融入解题各阶段，能够有效弥补单体LLM在教育场景中的不足，是实现负责任AI教育应用的有前景的路径。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of AI chatbots in education will drastically change learning, making responsible deployment a critical concern. While large language models (LLMs) might have access to sources discussing insights from educational sciences, they are not particularly inclined to adhere to pedagogical concepts, risking negative effects on the learning process, such as a loss of transfer capabilities, critical thinking, or creativity. In this paper, we introduce an agentic AI chatbot architecture assisting students with exercise solving, specifically designed to contribute to more responsible AI use in education. We base our conceptual development on the identification of several desiderata for responsible LLM-based educational systems, argue for the structural shortcomings inherent in monolithic, out-of-the-box solutions, and instead suggest modularizing the agentic architecture. We propose specific modules for different stages of exercise solving, enabling incorporation of targeted pedagogical advice, guiding students through the learning process in a more controllable, transparent, and overseeable manner.

</details>


### [226] [Double-Edged Sword or Sharp Tool? Designing and Evaluating Triadic LLM-Teacher Collaboration for K-12 Writing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30200)
*Canran Wang,Yuwen Yang,Zhen Wang,Ming Ma,Ding Yu,Chentai Wang,Keman Huang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过开发人机师三方协作系统，基于57,954篇K-12作文数据，验证了LLM作为生成引擎减轻教师负担、教师作为教学守门人保障反馈质量的分工模式，并发现过度语言扩展存在边际效用递减的 ceiling effect。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12教育中引入LLM需要有效的LLM-教师-学生三方协作机制，但目前缺乏大规模实证研究来验证这种协作的有效性及最优分工模式。

Method: 开发三方协作写作学习系统，基于系统功能语言学构建多维评估框架，结合建议轨迹追踪管线，收集10,195名学生在120所学校两年间的57,954篇作文进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 系统有效提升了写作质量；LLM作为生成引擎缓解了教师倦怠，教师作为教学守门人和桥梁保障了反馈质量；发现语言扩展存在ceiling effect，即过度扩展带来边际效用递减。

Conclusion: LLM和教师对学生技能提升均至关重要，但需根据学生能力水平动态调整LLM与教师的协作方式，采用自适应的协作策略。

Abstract: The double-edged sword of integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) requires an effective triadic collaboration mechanism among LLMs, teachers and students, especially for K-12 education. By developing a triadic collaboration system to support K-12 writing learning, a multidimensional evaluation framework grounded in Systemic Functional Linguistics and the suggestion trajectory tracing pipeline, this paper contributes a large-scale empirical dataset involving $57,954$ essays from $10,195$ students across $120$ schools over two years. Our findings confirm the efficacy of this system in improving writing quality through a strategic labor division: the LLM serves as a generative engine to mitigate teacher burnout, and the teacher acts as a pedagogical gatekeeper and bridge to guarantee feedback quality. While both LLM and teacher are critical for skill improvement, we uncover a ceiling effect where excessive linguistic expansion yields diminishing marginal utility. These suggest a dynamically adaptive LLM-teacher collaboration as student proficiency increases.

</details>


### [227] [When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219)
*Haoming Xu,Weihong Xu,Zongrui Li,Mengru Wang,Yunzhi Yao,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文信念管理（CBM）框架来评估和提升语言模型在长程交互中的状态更新、保持与忽略能力，通过BeliefTrack基准测试发现强化学习和表征引导能显著降低模型在信念管理中的失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程交互中语言模型需要管理累积信息，但现有模型在何时更新状态、保持状态或忽略无关信息方面存在严重缺陷，缺乏可量化的评估方法。

Method: 提出CBM框架与BeliefTrack基准（包括规则发现和电路诊断任务），使用有限信念空间和符号验证器进行逐轮评估；对比了基础模型、显式信念追踪提示、强化学习（信念状态奖励）和表征引导等方法。

Result: 基础模型存在严重的CBM失败（Failed Stay/Update/Isolation）；显式提示改进有限；强化学习平均降低失败率70.9%；表征引导降低失败率46.1%。

Conclusion: 强化学习和表征层面的引导是提升语言模型上下文信念管理的有效途径，揭示了潜伏的信念状态动态是失败的主要原因。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions require language models to manage accumulating information: when to update their state, when to preserve their state, and what to ignore. We study this challenge as \textbf{Contextual Belief Management (CBM)}: maintaining a predicted belief state aligned with formal evidence while isolating task-irrelevant noise. To make CBM measurable, we introduce BeliefTrack, a closed-world benchmark spanning Rule Discovery and Circuit Diagnosis, where a finite belief space and symbolic verifiers enable exact turn-level evaluation. BeliefTrack diagnoses three failures: Failed Stay, Failed Update, and Failed Isolation. Across multiple LLMs, vanilla models exhibit severe CBM failures, while explicit belief-tracking prompts provide limited gains. In contrast, reinforcement learning with belief-state rewards reduces failure rates by 70.9\% on average. Further probing reveals latent belief-state dynamics behind these failures, and representation-level steering reduces failure rates by 46.1\% across two tasks\footnote{Code is coming soon at https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM.

</details>


### [228] [mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30283)
*Peter W. Rose,Benjamin M. Good,Amanda M. Saravia-Butler,Charlotte A. Nelson,James P. Balhoff,Yaphet Kebede,Patricia L. Whetzel,Christopher Bizon,Andrew I. Su,Sergio E. Baranzini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: mcp-proto-okn 是一个基于 Python 的 Model Context Protocol 服务器，通过自然语言使 AI 助手能够发现、检查、查询和整合科学知识图谱，支持图路由、模式检查、SPARQL 执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成，降低了生物医学和科学用户跨域知识图谱分析的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低生物医学和科学用户进行跨域知识图谱分析的门槛，使非技术用户也能通过自然语言与科学知识图谱交互。

Method: 基于 Python 的 FastMCP 框架实现 Model Context Protocol 服务器，提供图路由、模式检查、SPARQL 执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成等功能。

Result: 实现了 mcp-proto-okn 服务器，使 AI 助手能够通过自然语言发现、检查、查询和整合科学知识图谱。

Conclusion: mcp-proto-okn 为科学知识图谱的跨域分析提供了一个低门槛的自然语言交互接口，代码和文档已在 GitHub 开源。

Abstract: MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users.
  mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [229] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个渐进式信息揭示的基准框架，用于评估LLM在科学发现和创新推理中的表现。该框架从仅提供研究主题和问题开始，逐步增加技术细节，在每个阶段评估模型生成的假设与原始论文结论的语义相似度。在45篇论文、多种先进模型上的实验表明，GPT-5.4即使在最少上下文下也能保持0.7的F1一致性分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估LLM在多跳检索方面的深度研究能力，但未能测试其在科学发现中所需的创新推理能力。作者希望填补这一空白，系统评估LLM从零信息到完整信息下的科学推理与发现能力。

Method: 构建渐进式信息揭示框架：模型初始仅接收论文主题和研究问题，技术细节逐步披露。在每个阶段，模型需生成假设，通过原子声明分解与原始论文结论进行语义相似度比较，评估从创新性（信息最少时）到有依据推理（信息完整时）的语义发散度。

Result: 在45篇论文（涵盖生物活性材料、机械材料、纳米材料）上评估了GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro preview。GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro优于各自前代；GPT-5.4即使在最少上下文下也能达到0.7的F1对齐分数。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统评估LLM的科学推理与发现能力奠定了基础，对开发下一代AI科学家/联合科学家系统至关重要。渐进式评估能同时衡量模型的创新能力和基于证据的推理能力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.

</details>


### [230] [MIRA: Mid-training Rubric Anchoring for Source-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30288)
*Haowen Wang,Yaxin Du,Jian Yang,Jiajun Wu,Shukai Liu,Yuxuan Zhang,Pingjie Wang,Siheng Chen,Tuney Zheng,Ming Zhou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA是一个基于自锚定规则发现的源感知过滤框架，通过先为每个数据源组发现评估规则，再蒸馏为可扩展的学生评分器进行全语料过滤，在代码中训练阶段仅用一半token即匹配全语料性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中训练阶段的数据选择问题与预训练和后训练不同：数据规模接近预训练但目标针对下游能力，且来自异构源（不同格式和训练角色）。现有方法要么可扩展但只提供隐式质量信号，要么语义选择强但假设固定规则或标准化格式，存在方法不匹配问题。

Method: 提出MIRA框架，核心是使规则构建成为数据选择的一部分：(1) 先为每个源组自动发现应评估的规则（自锚定规则发现）；(2) 将这些判断蒸馏为可扩展的学生评分器，用于全语料过滤。

Result: 在涉及21个数据源和5个源组的代码中训练场景中，MIRA在9个代码基准测试上优于所有选择基线，且仅用一半token即匹配全语料训练性能。

Conclusion: MIRA有效解决了中训练阶段数据选择的源感知和语义标准需求，证明了将规则构建融入数据选择流程的可行性。

Abstract: Mid-training has become an important stage in modern LLM development, using large-scale curated mixtures to strengthen capabilities before final post-training. Its data selection problem is distinct: the data are optimized under a pretraining-style objective at near-pretraining scale, but are curated toward downstream capabilities and drawn from heterogeneous sources with different formats and training roles. As a result, effective selection requires both scalability and source-adaptive semantic criteria. Existing model-based methods scale well, but provide only implicit quality signals. Semantic selection methods offer stronger judgments, but usually assume fixed rubrics or standardized data formats. To address this mismatch, we propose MIRA, a source-aware filtering framework based on self-anchored rubric discovery. The key idea is to make rubric construction part of data selection: MIRA first discovers what should be evaluated for each source group, then distills those judgments into scalable student scorers for full-corpus filtering. On code-oriented mid-training with 21 sources and 5 source groups, MIRA outperforms selection baselines across nine code benchmarks and matches the full-corpus run while using only half the tokens.

</details>


### [231] [Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30334)
*Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Tongshen Yang,Yonghan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wenshan Wu,Qihao Zhao,Hao Li,Yuanyuan Gao,Kim-Hui Yap,Scarlett Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了数据组织方式对LLM训练效率的影响，提出了四项关键准则（边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性、局部多样性），并基于此设计了两种新颖的数据排序方法STR和SAW，实验证明这些方法能有效提升训练稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练效率严重依赖数据质量，现有研究多关注数据选择（选哪些数据），而数据组织（数据如何排序）这一影响训练效果的关键因素尚未被充分探索，尤其是在LLM通常只训练1-2个epoch的背景下。

Method: 利用已有数据效率研究中预计算的样本级分数（无需额外计算开销），系统探索数据组织的影响。形式化提出四项准则：边界锐化（Batch内数据难度一致）、循环调度（难度循环变化）、课程连续性（相邻Batch难度渐进）、局部多样性（Batch内数据多样性）。基于这些准则设计了STR和SAW两种数据排序方法，在预训练和SFT阶段、不同模型规模和不同数据量下进行验证。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的四项准则的有效性，以及STR和SAW方法在提升LLM训练稳定性和性能方面的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 数据组织方式对LLM训练有显著影响，通过合理的排序策略可以在几乎不增加计算开销的前提下提升训练效果，为未来的数据效率研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead. We identify and formalize four key guidelines for optimizing data organization: Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity. Guided by them, we introduce two novel data ordering methods termed STR and SAW. Extensive experiments across different model scales and data sizes, encompassing both pre-training and SFT stages, validate the effectiveness of our summarized guidelines. They also demonstrate the robustness of our proposed data ordering methods in enhancing the stability and performance of LLM training. Github Link: https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/

</details>


### [232] [Locally Coherent, Globally Incoherent: Bounding Compositional Incoherence in Multi-Component LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30335)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了多组件LLM智能体中“局部一致但全局不一致”的失败模式，提出了组合残差ε*的度量方法、乘积结构二分法、Rayleigh商预测、Boyle-Dykstra投影修复以及时序一致性监控方法，并通过大量实验验证了该问题的普遍性和现有缓解措施的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组件LLM智能体在联合问题上，每个组件仅观察部分信息并输出概率性断言，这些局部一致的断言通过组合后可能违反基本概率公理，导致全局不一致。现有研究缺乏对这种失败模式的严格形式化描述、度量方法和修复机制。

Method: 1) 定义组合残差ε*（组合输出到联合一致多面体的L2距离）；2) 乘积结构二分法刻画局部一致性何时能保证全局一致；3) Rayleigh商公式预测残差大小；4) 层次化Boyle-Dykstra投影进行确定性修复；5) 任意时有效的e-过程实现序列化一致性监控。

Result: 在1,876个集成团（四LLM中端面板）上，33-94%的团ε*>0；在1,770个已结算赌注上，按比例分配规则每个赌注产生+0.115 nats的遗憾（当投注者自身进行一致化处理后降至+0.006）。Rayleigh商预测在四个关系类中的三个上，与观测残差的匹配误差在7%以内。

Conclusion: 多组件LLM智能体的全局不一致问题普遍存在且不可忽视；三种直观的LLM侧缓解措施（检索、分区感知提示、聚合器LLM）均失败或导致性能倒退；层次化投影修复在理论上有效。

Abstract: Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.

</details>


### [233] [Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30344)
*Xiaona Zhou,Muntasir Wahed,Tianjiao Yu,Constantin Brif,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VisAnomBench基准数据集和VisAnomReasoner模型，通过微调视觉-语言模型实现时间序列异常检测的可解释决策，在精确率和F1上分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在多模态异常检测任务中表现不佳，且公开异常检测基准仅提供区间标注而缺乏自然语言解释，难以微调VLM以生成可解释的、有依据的决策。

Method: 从公开时间序列数据集构建VisAnomBench基准，利用多个大型VLM结合细粒度任务特定奖励筛选高质量异常解释，在此基础上对VLM进行参数高效微调，得到VisAnomReasoner模型。

Result: 在VisAnomBench上，VisAnomReasoner在精确率和F1上分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点；在TSB-AD-U基准上，精确率和F1分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点，展现出强跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: VisAnomReasoner通过构建高质量带解释的基准数据集和参数高效微调，显著提升了时间序列异常检测的准确性和可解释性，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [234] [SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30345)
*Qinpei Luo,Ruichun Ma,Xinyu Zhang,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SchGen，首个将自然语言需求转化为可编辑PCB原理图的大语言模型。通过设计语义化的代码表示和构建大规模数据集，解决了原理图生成中表示复杂、数据缺乏的难题，在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB原理图设计目前仍依赖人工和专业知识，尽管生成式AI在数字/模拟IC设计领域取得了进展，但面向自然语言需求的PCB原理图自动生成几乎未被探索。现有原理图格式存在工具特定语法冗长、几何描述繁重的问题，难以被LLM可靠生成。

Method: 1) 提出语义化的代码表示（semantically grounded code representation），将原理图编辑原语编码为相对布局和基于引脚名的连线，将几何驱动的生成问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务；2) 构建人机协同的数据流水线，将开源硬件设计转换为此表示，形成大规模PCB原理图-用户提示配对数据集；3) 基于此训练SchGen大语言模型。

Result: SchGen在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于替代表示方法和更大规模的通用LLM，验证了表示设计在复杂硬件设计生成任务中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 表示设计对于使生成式模型胜任复杂硬件设计任务至关重要，SchGen证明了面向LLM的语义化代码表示是PCB原理图自动生成的有效路径。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现ROME和MEMIT编辑方法虽然修改不同事实，但都针对相同的权重子集，并通过训练一个二进制掩码验证了这一共同功能结构，进而揭示了编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识来运作的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法主要通过输出行为评估，其内部机制尚未得到充分探索。本文旨在探究这些编辑方法是否依赖于某种通用机制，无论修改的是哪个事实。

Method: 训练一个紧凑的二进制掩码来隔离编辑后权重的关键子集，通过该掩码在不同编辑上的逆转效果（训练集80%、测试集70%以上）来验证共同功能结构的存在。通过分析掩码对注意力分布的影响以及注入掩码对编辑成功率的影响来揭示机制。

Result: 1) 掩码能逆转超过70%的编辑（测试集），证明不同编辑共享共同功能结构；2) 掩码通过消除后层的过度注意力来逆转编辑；3) 编辑过程中注入掩码使编辑成功率从98%降至38%，表明该机制对编辑成功是必要的。

Conclusion: 编辑机制是抑制而非覆盖知识，这解释了ROME和MEMIT无法将更改传播到相关事实的原因。发现的共同功能子空间可用于检测和防御不期望的编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [236] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐与表征动态。利用TradeArena测试平台，发现市场压力下规划嵌入漂移、计划-风险表征分离、有效秩收缩等预失败信号，验证了这些信号对嵌入选择、噪声和虚假审计报告的鲁棒性。结构化风险反馈可作为外部对齐信号但非通用性能增强器，最后通过51只股票日内实验揭示LLM理由与风险层之间的相关性盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM智能体在金融决策中何时对齐、漂移或失败缺乏系统性理解，尤其是在市场压力下其行为表征的动态变化尚不明确。本文旨在通过可审计的交易环境揭示LLM金融推理失败前的可测量信号。

Method: 基于TradeArena可审计交易智能体测试平台，包含风险报告、执行模拟、记忆和可回放轨迹。分析规划嵌入漂移、计划-风险表征分离、流形有效秩收缩。采用80个滚动失败锚点覆盖8条LLM轨迹，使用哈希、LSA、Transformer和白盒隐藏状态探针进行验证。通过无思维链权重、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声和虚假审计报告进行压力测试，最后进行51只股票日内实验并引入滚动Markowitz基线作为协方差参考。

Result: 1) 发现市场压力下规划嵌入偏离正常状态质心、融合计划-风险表征区分正常与预回撤状态、有效秩收缩等预失败信号，且收缩效应跨不同嵌入方法持续存在；2) 压力测试显示：无推理链时理由级收缩消失但意图空间收缩保留，词汇多样性未崩溃，融合信号在噪声下保持信息量；3) 结构化风险反馈可作为外部对齐信号但非通用性能增强器，真实审计反馈对不同模型产生不同效果；4) 51只股票实验揭示LLM理由常为耦合资产的集中暴露提供合理化，而风险层反复对其进行削减。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈和表征轨迹能够揭示LLM金融推理何时对齐、漂移或失败。本文支持研究声明而非盈利声明：这些信号可用于监控LLM智能体的行为健康状况，但不保证交易盈利。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [237] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"差分电路脆弱性"指标，从机制层面比较RL和SFT对LLM内部计算电路的保留程度，发现SFT适应任务更快但导致更大的电路破坏和灾难性遗忘，而RL更好地保留基础电路但任务适应较慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作从行为层面发现RL比SFT更能缓解灾难性遗忘，但缺乏对内部计算电路保留程度的机制性理解。本文旨在从机制层面探究RL的优势是否源于对内部计算电路的更好保留。

Method: 提出差分电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability），一种基于注意力头的度量方法，用于衡量微调过程中特定电路退化的程度。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上，针对科学问答任务，对比RL和SFT两种微调策略的电路保留情况。

Result: 发现明显的机制性权衡：SFT能更快适应目标任务，但导致更大的电路破坏和先前能力的遗忘；RL则保留了更大比例的基础电路，但任务适应速度较慢。

Conclusion: 电路保留程度可能是解释RL相比SFT更能抵抗灾难性遗忘的机制性原因。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [238] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一个基于LLM推理的轨迹感知分子先导物优化智能体，将工具选择建模为序列决策问题，在ADMET优化任务上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子先导物优化方法多采用单步优化，忽略了序列设计决策的长期影响，无法实现前瞻性的结构优化。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，利用大语言模型作为推理引擎，将分子优化工具的选择建模为动作轨迹上的序列决策问题，实现对结构约束下的前向优化。

Result: 在多个ADMET优化任务上，TRACE相比基线模型取得了更高的优化成功率、更大的性质改善幅度和更高的分子有效性，同时保持了分子相似性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的序列决策方法能有效提升先导物优化效果，为AI辅助药物发现提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [239] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四人非完全信息扑克游戏Big 2中构建自强化学习框架，系统比较策略梯度与值函数近似方法。PPO在对抗随机、贪婪和启发式对手时优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q学习。适度熵正则化通过防止策略过度确定性提升PPO性能，当前策略自对弈比检查点自对弈或固定对手训练提供更强的有限预算课程。Big 2是研究深度强化学习在非完全信息、多智能体交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集问题上的有效受控环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非完全信息多人博弈中智能体在隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手条件下的行为表现，并验证Big 2作为深度强化学习受控实验环境的有效性。

Method: 构建Big 2的自对弈强化学习框架，在统一环境、输入表示、训练预算和评估协议下，比较PPO与MC Q近似、SARSA、Q-learning的性能；引入熵正则化防止策略过确定性；对比当前策略自对弈、检查点自对弈和固定对手训练的课程效果。

Result: PPO在对抗随机、贪婪和启发式对手时显著优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning；适度熵正则化提升PPO性能；当前策略自对弈提供更强的有限预算课程。

Conclusion: Big 2是研究深度强化学习在非完全信息、多人交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集条件下的有效受控实验平台。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [240] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于VAE的世界模型在随机具身探索中，其潜在空间会自发形成与物理几何结构对应的空间语义结构，且预测性能与语义对齐共同提升。通过双重敲除实验（标准KL正则化）同时破坏两者，降低beta值则同时恢复，证实物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有语言监督的情况下，世界模型从物理探索中学到的表征的底层组织原则是什么。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型进行随机具身探索，分析潜在空间的结构；通过20个时间检查点分析预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系；进行双重敲除实验（调整KL正则化beta值）验证共享驱动假说。

Result: 方向准确率0.677±0.029（随机编码器0.547），位置RSA 0.192±0.047（随机编码器0.029，提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐显著共变（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）；标准KL正则化（beta=0.1）在第50,000步同时破坏两者，beta=0.001则同时恢复。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则，这一发现对设计具有语义基础的具身智能体有直接启示。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [241] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COM策略，通过在初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束来保持时间序列token的连续性和序数性，从而提升基于token的时间序列大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的时间序列大语言模型（TS-LLMs）忽视了时间序列token固有的连续性和序数性，这严重限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter），一种感知连续性和序数性的策略，在token嵌入的初始化和训练阶段集成几何约束。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上，COM一致地提升了基于token的TS-LLMs的性能，取得了有竞争力的结果和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保持时间序列token嵌入的连续性和序数性对于基于token的TS-LLMs的有效性至关重要，COM策略有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [242] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow提出了一种基于Koopman动力学的专家流匹配方法，通过置信度感知的胜者全得机制和残差动力学校正，解决了标准流匹配在多模态多尺度时间序列生成中的频谱收缩和模式覆盖不足问题，在多项基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学（如振荡和高频变化），标准流匹配方法使用单一全局向量场估计器，在 heterogeneous 时间分布中不同模态可能经过相近流状态但需要不兼容的条件速度，导致估计器学习到过度平滑的局部传输场近似，进而产生频谱失真和模式覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PrismFlow，包含三个核心组件：(1) Koopman启发的动力学专家——每个专家在潜在空间中学习残差校正，将局部非线性时间演化近似为线性过渡；(2) 置信度感知的Winner-Take-All (WTA)目标——仅更新与每个样本最对齐的专家，对其余专家梯度掩码，鼓励模态特异性专化；(3) 采样时，所选专家向全局传输场添加残差动力学校正，在保持流匹配稳定性的同时恢复细粒度高频时间结构。

Result: 在多项基准上达到SOTA性能，Context-FID提升15.6%，Discriminative Score改进38.6%，同时在低数据场景保持鲁棒性，并有效支持时间序列预测和缺失值插补任务。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过多专家协作的流匹配框架有效缓解了标准流匹配中的频谱收缩问题，能够同时恢复多模态模式和高频时间结构，在生成质量、模式覆盖和下游任务适用性上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [243] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出平衡多模态标签重塑方法（BMLR），从标签角度解决多模态学习中因模态映射难度差异导致的学习速度不均衡问题，实验表明该方法能有效提升多模态性能并兼容多种模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过增强弱模态或调整优化梯度来缓解模态不平衡，但往往牺牲强模态的优化能力，且未从模态层面分析学习速度差异的根源。

Method: 提出BMLR方法，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态的映射难度，从而促进模态交互并注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的大量实验表明，BMLR持续提升了多模态性能，并与不同模型设计具有强兼容性。

Conclusion: 多模态学习速度差异源于各模态特征空间到共享标签空间映射难度的不同，BMLR从标签侧设计有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [244] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏置和噪声的三部分统计框架研究柏拉图表征假说（PRH），提出线性表征假说（LRH）是跨模态对齐的信号基础，通过稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征可增强对齐；偏置可通过中心化和归一化部分缓解；噪声由数据稀缺驱动，词频与对齐度呈正相关。最终提出改进LRH的统计模型解释现代AI架构中的表征对齐现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索柏拉图表征假说（PRH）的统计基础，即不同AI模型为何会收敛到相似的表征空间，并解释其背后的信号、偏置和噪声机制。

Method: 提出信号、偏置、噪声三部分统计框架：1）信号部分基于线性表征假说（LRH），使用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征；2）偏置部分通过中心化和归一化缓解模型差异；3）噪声部分分析词频与对齐度的相关性，并建立统计模型。

Result: 稀疏表征比密集表征具有更强的跨模态对齐能力；中心化和归一化能一致提升跨模型对齐；词频与对齐度存在强正相关，表明数据稀缺驱动表征噪声。

Conclusion: 提出一个改进线性表征假说的统计模型，能够解释不同现代AI架构中表征对齐的多种现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [245] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对量化基准的最小可检测效应（MDE）边界公式，将经典配对二项样本量计算方法适配到量化评估场景，并通过未配对的试点审计验证，发现n=100子样本上的大部分"基准不可靠性"方差实际上是二项抽样噪声，且提示模板变异远大于量化噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准的可靠性缺乏量化标准，研究者无法区分报告的性能差异是源于量化方法本身还是评估过程中的随机噪声。

Method: 将Miettinen(1968)的配对二项样本量计算适配到量化基准场景，推导出保守的最小可检测效应下界δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)；在4个模型和4个基准上进行了k=5次分裂的n=100试点审计；并设计了并行的MMLU提示模板研究以比较量化噪声与提示噪声的规模。

Result: 在假设ρ_d=0.10时，所有观测到的NF4-FP16差值均低于隐含MDE；多数跨分裂标准差在二项参考值√(p(1-p)/n)的±1.5个百分点内；单个边界案例(OPT-WinoGrande, |Δ|=3.2 pp)在ρ_d=0.10时低于MDE但在ρ_d=0.05时高于MDE；MMLU上提示模板变幅(2-10 pp)达到或超过最大量化差值(3.2 pp)。

Conclusion: 量化基准的大部分报告方差实为二项抽样噪声；提示模板变异必须预先固定，否则会淹没量化噪声；提供了一个五行的预注册模板供基准设计者使用。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [246] [A Theoretical and Experimental Study of a Novel Adaptive Learning Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29273)
*Sakshi Kumari,Shyam Kumar M,Sushmitha P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Adam和AMSGrad等自适应优化方法，并针对其局限性提出了一种基于视距（line of sight）方法的新优化器C-Adam，提供了收敛性理论证明并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应学习率优化器（如Adam）在实际任务中虽被广泛使用，但无法保证收敛，AMSGrad虽针对非收敛问题提出但仍存在局限，因此需要设计一种既能保证收敛又能减少计算开销和震荡的新优化器。

Method: 提出C-Adam优化器，基于视距（line of sight）方法改进自适应优化机制，并给出收敛性的理论证明。

Result: 通过多个真实数值实验验证了C-Adam的有效性，并提供了收敛性理论证明。

Conclusion: C-Adam在保证收敛性的同时能有效减少震荡和计算成本，是对Adam和AMSGrad的有效改进。

Abstract: A crucial component of machine learning algorithms is minimizing loss functions with less computational cost and less oscillations. While adaptive learning rate-based optimizers have been widely used for real-world tasks, they do not guarantee convergence, which is why AMSGrad was later introduced to investigate the non-convergence behaviour of Adam. In this paper, popular adaptive optimization methods like Adam and AMSGrad are critically reviewed with an emphasis on their fundamental design concepts. To address limitations of the above mentioned optimizers, a new optimizer variant, C-Adam, is proposed based on the line of sight approach. A theoretical proof for convergence is also provided and the optimizer is validated through a number of real-life based numerical experiments.

</details>


### [247] [Singularity-aware Optimization via Randomized Geometric Probing: Towards Stable Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29547)
*Ruoran Xu,Borong She,Xiaobo Jin,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam)优化器，通过局部几何不稳定性(LGI)度量动态调整步长，解决了非平滑损失景观下自适应优化器（如Adam）的梯度抖动问题，在量化感知训练和高噪声小批量学习任务中优于AdamW和Prox-SGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习优化依赖于损失景观平滑的假设，但现代架构中ReLU激活和量化算子等非平滑组件系统性违反了这一假设，导致Adam等自适应优化器出现梯度抖动（Clarke次微分内冲突信号引起的剧烈振荡），造成收敛性能差和泛化能力下降。

Method: 提出S-Adam优化器，核心是局部几何不稳定性(LGI)度量——通过随机方向导数的方差高效估计Clarke次微分直径，并引入自适应阻尼机制exp(-λρ)，在高不稳定区域减速更新，在平滑区域保持快速收敛。

Result: 在量化感知训练(QAT)和高噪声小批量学习任务中，S-Adam在CIFAR-100上提升6%准确率，在TinyImageNet上提升3%准确率，有效缓解了梯度振荡。

Conclusion: S-Adam在非平滑优化场景下性能领先，且具有严格收敛性证明（几乎必然收敛到(δ,ε)-Clarke稳定点，达到最优O(1/√T)收敛率）。

Abstract: Deep learning optimization relies heavily on the assumption of smooth loss landscapes, a condition systematically violated by modern architectures due to non-smooth components such as ReLU activations and quantization operators. In such non-smooth regimes, adaptive optimizers such as Adam suffer from gradient chattering, violent oscillations caused by conflicting signals within the Clarke subdifferential, leading to poor convergence and suboptimal generalization. To address this, we introduce Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam), a novel optimizer that stabilizes training by dynamically modulating step sizes based on local geometric instability. Our key contribution is the Local Geometric Instability (LGI) metric, a computationally efficient estimator of the Clarke subdifferential diameter derived from the variance of randomized directional derivatives. S-Adam incorporates an adaptive damping mechanism exp(-$λ$$ρ$) that decelerates updates in high-instability regions while preserving fast convergence in smooth basins. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>


### [248] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文蒸馏重新形式化为潜在记忆管理问题，通过将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器构建模块化记忆库，引入检索、路由和自门控机制实现记忆的选择性激活，并采用缓存共享提升推理效率。实验表明该方法显著优于基线检索方法，自门控机制通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了多个蒸馏后的潜在记忆应如何在非理想场景下存储、检索和安全激活的问题。

Method: 1) 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立的LoRA适配器，形成模块化记忆库；2) 输入查询时检索候选记忆，路由至最合适的适配器；3) 使用自门控(Self-Gating)机制决定是否激活潜在记忆；4) 引入缓存共享减少推理时的管理开销。

Result: 该方法显著优于带检索的基线方法，自门控机制通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提升了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏作为潜在记忆管理问题处理是有效的，模块化记忆库结合检索、路由和自门控机制可以在非理想场景下实现安全、高效的潜在记忆激活。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [249] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代深度学习缺乏对算法复杂度的归纳偏置，提出凯莱表补全作为离散代数的规范测试平台，并呼吁将连续平坦性先验推广以自主发现离散算法公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在连续域表现优异，但无法外推到精确的算法或离散代数规则，缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置。

Method: 将凯莱表补全作为矩阵补全的离散代数对应物；采用算子值张量分解结合平坦性先验，产生对离散结合律的隐式算法偏置。

Result: 算子值张量分解与平坦性先验结合可产生对离散结合律的隐式算法偏置；但凯莱表补全的精确恢复界尚未建立。

Conclusion: 提出了凯莱表补全的开放问题，挑战学界将连续平坦性先验推广到更广泛的离散算法公理自主发现，避免组合搜索。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [250] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）研究LoRA微调对大型语言模型表示几何的影响。通过引入delta激活框架隔离适配器贡献，发现LoRA诱导的特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间存在较弱的几何对齐，表明LoRA更新在残差流中占据部分独特的表示结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已被广泛用于大模型适配，但其内部表示变化机制尚未被充分理解，本文旨在探索LoRA微调诱导的表示几何特性。

Method: 引入delta激活框架隔离适配器贡献；在Gemma-2-9B上使用LoRA秩4/8/16/32训练适配器特定SAE；采用余弦相似度、主角度分析和CKA评估表示对齐。

Result: LoRA特征字典与预训练SAE特征几何对齐较弱；适配器特定SAE能更有效重建delta激活；特征密度随秩和深度增加，几何差异相对稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调会诱导预训练可解释性字典未能完全捕捉的特征结构，对机械可解释性、适配分析及微调模型安全审计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [251] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的固有几何结构控制扩散模型。通过条件期望算子的奇异函数学习低维特征表示，实现无需重训练或反向传播的稳定高保真控制。在CIFAR-10上条件准确率提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍，并揭示出引导有效性的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成过程中噪声逐渐破坏数据，仅有少量特征对控制仍然保持信息量。现有训练-free引导方法需要反向传播或辅助模型，效率低且稳定性差。作者希望利用生成过程的固有几何结构，找到这些信息丰富的低维特征，实现高效稳定的引导。

Method: 1) 将引导信号投影到条件期望算子的奇异函数张成的子空间上；2) 通过自监督目标学习这些奇异函数（无需配对数据）；3) 将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码）直接投影到采样轨迹上；4) 无需去噪器反向传播或重训练。

Result: 1) CIFAR-10上条件准确率比最强训练-free基线提升37个百分点；2) 采样速度提升4倍；3) 同一表示同时支持标签、CLIP和空间掩码引导，无需辅助模型；4) 发现生成过程中的相变，精确确定有效引导的最优时间窗口。

Conclusion: Spectral Guidance提供了一种统一、高效的扩散模型控制框架，通过几何本征结构实现多类型引导信号的高保真控制，揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，为理解扩散模型生成动力学提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [252] [Sequential Physics-Constrained Neural Operator Forward Modeling for the $\textit{Norne}$ Reservoir System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28909)
*Clement Etienam,Juntao Yang,Oleg Ovcharenko,Nick Luiken,Tsubasa Onishi,Nefeli Moridis,Issam Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了面向黑油模型三阶段动力学的神经算子序贯替代建模理论框架，重点研究傅立叶神经算子（FNO）及其物理信息变体（PINO），在Norne基准油藏（46×112×22网格，113,344个单元，30个时间步，3298天生产历史）上验证。理论贡献涵盖四个层面：乘积Sobolev空间中的泛函分析（隐式时间步映射适定性与局部Lipschitz估计）、协变量偏移量化（Wasserstein-2距离指数增长界）、物理约束谱稳定性（PINO训练降低Jacobian谱半径，实现时间一致 rollout 误差），以及K步TBPTT梯度分析（几何偏置衰减与Adam收敛）。实验证实：自回归PINO替代模型在全3298天范围内维持油相R²>0.99、气相R²>0.90、压力R²≈0.80、水相R²单调提升；8块NVIDIA B200 GPU训练<1小时；单B200 GPU上1000成员集合模拟<1分钟，比OPM有限体积模拟器实现约10⁴倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限体积数值模拟器（如OPM）在高分辨率油藏模拟中计算代价高昂，尤其在需要大规模集合模拟（如不确定性量化、历史匹配）时面临瓶颈。本文旨在建立严格的理论框架，证明神经算子（特别是PINO）能够以可证明的精度和稳定性替代传统求解器，实现几个数量级的加速，同时保证长期自回归rollout的数值稳定性。

Method: (1) 将三阶段黑油模型隐式时间步映射表述为乘积Sobolev空间中的算子学习问题；(2) 采用FNO/PINO架构学习状态变量（油、气、水饱和度及压力）的时间演化映射；(3) 理论分析包括：泛函分析适定性、Wasserstein距离下的协变量偏移边界、谱半径约束的物理正则化、TBPTT梯度截断的几何偏置衰减；(4) 在Norne基准油藏上进行实证验证，与OPM有限体积求解器对比。

Result: 自回归PINO替代模型在3298天全时间跨度上：油相R²>0.99，气相R²>0.90，压力R²≈0.80，水相R²单调提升。8×B200 GPU训练<1小时。单GPU上1000成员集合<1分钟，相比OPM求解器实现约10⁴倍加速。所有理论预测（谱稳定性、误差界、梯度偏置衰减）均得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了神经算子用于油藏模拟替代建模的完整理论框架，证明了PINO在长期自回归模拟中具有可证明的稳定性与精度。所提出的框架为大规模油藏不确定性量化、历史匹配和优化提供了一条切实可行的计算路径，理论分析为后续神经算子替代建模研究提供了严格的基础。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive mathematical and computational framework for sequential surrogate modeling of three-phase black-oil reservoir dynamics using neural operators, with particular emphasis on Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) and their physics-informed variant (PINO). The application focus is the Norne benchmark reservoir, defined on a heterogeneous $46\times112\times22$ grid ($N=113,344$ cells), with a production history spanning $T=30$ timesteps covering 3298 days. Our theoretical contributions are organized around four interlocking problems: (1) functional-analytic formulation in a product-Sobolev-space setting, including well-posedness of the implicit timestep map and sharp local Lipschitz estimates; (2) covariate shift quantification, proving that the Wasserstein-2 distance grows as $W_2 \leq \varepsilon(L^n-1)/(L-1)$, with exponential population-risk discrepancy for $L>1$; (3) physics-constrained spectral stability, showing PINO training with $λ_R \geq λ^*_R$ reduces the learned Jacobian spectral radius to $ρ_F + Cλ_R^{-1/2}$, yielding uniform-in-time rollout error $|δ_n| \leq \varepsilon/(1-ρ)$; and (4) $K$-step TBPTT gradient analysis, deriving geometric bias decay $O(ρ^K)$, optimal window $K^ = O(\log(T/σ^2))$, and Adam convergence $O(1/\sqrt{t}) + O(ρ^{K^*})$. Empirical validation confirms all theoretical predictions: autoregressive PINO surrogates sustain $R^2>0.99$ (oil), $R^2>0.90$ (gas), $R^2\approx 0.80$ (pressure), and monotonically improving $R^2$ (water) across the full 3298-day horizon, trained on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs in under one hour. A 1000-member ensemble runs in under one minute on a single B200 GPU, giving a ${\sim}10^4\times$ wall-clock speedup over the OPM finite-volume simulator.

</details>


### [253] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM生成奖励塑形在稀疏结构化强化学习任务中的应用，提出将奖励函数生成视为调试过程而非一次性生成。通过诊断驱动的迭代精炼方法，在MiniGrid任务中取得了显著提升（DoorKey-8x8从2.3%到97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%到86.7%），并分析了失败模式、控制实验和方法的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的奖励塑形方法多采用一次性生成（one-shot generation），但在稀疏结构化任务中效果不稳定。作者认为应将其重新定位为调试过程（debugging），即通过训练诊断信息迭代修正奖励函数，以提升LLM生成奖励函数的可靠性和有效性。

Method: 以MiniGrid为核心评估环境、MuJoCo为边界压力测试，使用PPO训练智能体。首先审计发现两种主要的一次性失败模式：奖励泛滥（reward flooding）和语义/API误解，以及较罕见的弱塑形（weak-shaping）案例。提出诊断驱动的迭代精炼方法：基于训练诊断结果和失败模式分类法，指导目标奖励函数的针对性修订。控制实验包括：仅指标重新提示、静态词汇表控制、预算匹配、Best-of-3比较、组件移除测试、敏感性分析等。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%，但种子间方差较高。控制实验表明：仅指标重新提示导致大幅下降；静态词汇表控制恢复大部分差距（87.6%；70.7%），说明分类法提示是主要机制，动态标签仅提供部分增量证据。Budget-matched和Best-of-3比较分离了精炼与选择/训练时间效应。连续控制结果显示边界条件：成功导向的诊断在密集奖励运动任务中可能误报。

Conclusion: LLM生成奖励塑形更适合作为调试过程而非一次性生成。该方法适用于PPO下具有可靠接口的稀疏结构化任务，但在密集奖励连续控制场景中效果有限。低调用协议是相对于基于种群的奖励搜索的成本对比，而非基准比较。在四个交叉方差设计环境中，点估计表明当LLM奖励函数方差占主导时增益更大，但bootstrap区间较宽。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [254] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CosmicFish-HRM是一个紧凑型语言模型，通过层级推理模块（HRM）实现自适应推理深度，根据输入复杂度动态分配计算资源，在推理时采用非均匀推理步数，展示了自适应推理深度作为参数规模扩展的替代方案的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自适应推理深度这一方向，以替代依赖大规模参数和昂贵推理的传统方法，旨在紧凑模型中实现高效推理能力。

Method: 构建Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM)，在推理时通过高低两个层次的推理循环迭代，根据输入复杂度动态决定何时停止；结合Grouped Query Attention、RoPE、SwiGLU等现代Transformer组件。

Result: 模型学会了非均匀推理行为，不同任务和输入分配不同的推理步数；小规模下推理基础设施带来开销，但随模型规模增长，HRM核心的相对成本降低，该权衡变得更有利。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可作为依赖参数规模扩展的有前景的替代方案，为紧凑型语言模型提供推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [255] [A Training-Time Diagnostic for Generalization via the Log-Alignment Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28975)
*Ali Shehper,Ashish Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了log-alignment ratio (LAR)，将其表示为权重谱与激活谱之间的重叠，并证明该指标能够有效追踪从记忆到泛化的转变过程。在grokking现象中，LAR可用于预测学习函数的有效维度；在大型语言模型预训练中，LAR的偏差能够追踪泛化差距，且其下降速率可预示过拟合的临近。LAR仅需前向传播中即可计算，计算开销极小，且无需验证集数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数化理论中提出的log-alignment ratio (LAR)指标，将其重新形式化为权重谱与激活谱之间的重叠，并验证其在不同训练场景下追踪记忆与泛化之间转变的能力。

Method: 将LAR重新定义为权重谱p（矩阵归一化平方奇异值的分布）与激活谱q（输入在其奇异方向上的归一化平方投影的分布）之间的重叠。通过在grokking和3B参数语言模型预训练两种设置下对LAR进行实证分析，研究其与泛化行为的关系。

Result: 在grokking中，LAR可预测学习函数的有效维度$k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$；在3B语言模型预训练中，LAR与泛化差距之间存在可追踪的偏差关系，且其下降速率随过拟合临近而增加。

Conclusion: LAR是一个高效的、无需验证数据的指标，能够有效追踪模型从记忆到泛化的转变过程，并预测过拟合的发生。

Abstract: We study the log-alignment ratio (LAR), a measure of parameter-activation alignment, introduced in parameterization theory. We reformulate it as the overlap between a weight spectrum $p$ of the normalized squared singular values of a matrix and an activation spectrum $q$ of the normalized squared projections of inputs onto its singular directions. We show that unembedding LAR tracks the transition between memorization and generalization in two different settings by capturing the spread of $p$ and $q$ during training. In grokking, LAR predicts the effective dimension of the learned function: $k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$, where $n$ is the input dimension of the matrix. In 3B-parameter language model pre-training, its deviation from a non-overfitting baseline tracks the generalization gap, and its rate of decline increases as overfitting approaches. LAR is computable from quantities available during the forward pass with negligible computational overhead, and requires no held-out validation data.

</details>


### [256] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在InceptionTime架构上应用多种后验可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）分析基于EEG的抑郁症检测模型的决策过程，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）存在部分一致的归因模式，但方法间存在显著差异，强调了后验可解释性在精神疾病EEG分类中的潜力与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在基于EEG的抑郁症检测中表现优异，但其高容量模型的决策过程难以解释，缺乏对模型归因机制的系统性理解。

Method: 采用InceptionTime架构进行MDD分类，在subject-level分层5折交叉验证框架下，对Shapley方法（DeepSHAP）、梯度方法（Integrated Gradients、GradCAM）和扰动方法（Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）进行全局归因聚合分析。

Result: 不同可解释性方法在额叶、颞叶和后部EEG区域（尤其是右半球）呈现部分一致的归因模式；梯度法和扰动法之间一致性较高，而DeepSHAP的归因分布明显不同；方法间差异反映了方法论假设对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 后验可解释性方法捕捉到EEG深度学习模型中部分重叠的相关性结构，归因模式与既往MDD相关EEG研究大致一致，但应视为探索性分析而非神经生理学生物标志物或临床适用性的确定性证据。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [257] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSimSiam是一个轻量级自监督表征学习框架，通过正样本对学习鲁棒且可泛化的fMRI特征，在数据有限的情况下超越全监督基线，逼近大规模模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据采集成本高、样本量小，且精神科量表存在主观性导致标签质量不一，加之fMRI数据高维度特性，容易导致模型过拟合。现有fMRI基础模型需要大量计算资源进行预训练和微调，门槛过高。

Method: 提出BrainSimSiam，一种数据高效的自监督表征学习框架，利用仅含正样本的数据对（positive-only pairs）学习鲁棒且可泛化的特征表征。

Result: 在多个下游分类和回归任务上取得强性能，超越全监督基线，逼近大规模模型的表现。

Conclusion: 轻量级自监督框架能够生成可跨多种下游任务泛化的表征，为数据受限的神经影像应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [258] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对MathCheck（ICLR 2025）的改写质量审计发现3.1%的语义错误改写，移除后GPT-4o排名从第2降至第4；跨模型一致性检测可自动发现这些错误（成本<$10）。进一步发现Claude Haiku 4.5准确率86%但语义一致性率仅50%，9个模型准确率跨度仅86-96%但SCR跨度达50-82%。形式化证明存在"无免费基准"推论，并提出FormInv审计协议和SCR等度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试存在两重问题：一是自动生成的改写（如连词变体）可能语义不正确，二是即使语义正确的改写，不同模型在等价表述下的回答也不一致（语义不一致性）。这些缺陷导致模型排名对改写选择敏感，且标准准确率指标无法反映这种不一致性。

Method: 对MathCheck审计发现3.1%语义错误；提出跨模型多数一致性（≥3/4或≥6/9）自动检测；对9个前沿模型在Lean4验证定理上计算语义一致性率（SCR）和每定理Cochran's Q；形式化证明无免费基准推论。

Result: MathCheck中3.1%语义错误改写；GPT-4o排名从第2降至第4；Claude Haiku 4.5准确率86%但SCR仅50%；9模型准确率96-96%但SCR 50-82%；存在32个百分点的差距；任何排名都存在对应的改写权重实现。

Conclusion: 标准基准测试的准确率指标掩盖了重要的语义不一致性问题；模型排名对改写选择高度敏感；需要将语义一致性率作为额外评估维度；FormInv提供了可复现的审计协议和度量工具。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [259] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRe是一种无需训练、推理时即插即用的包装器，通过动态路由计算到高冲突/高不确定性的交互上，每步只评估固定比例的交互，从而大幅提升扩散组合优化求解器的可扩展性。在MIS问题上，推理规模扩展3倍以上，实现~8倍加速和~12倍峰值内存降低；在TSP问题上，n=1000时实现~15倍加速和44倍内存降低，解质量保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器每次迭代都要重新评估稠密的边/因子交互，导致推理在墙钟时间上昂贵且在大规模时受内存瓶颈限制。

Method: 受多体物理学计算方法的启发，LoRe作为一个无需训练、推理时即插即用的包装器，强制执行每步交互评估预算：每次迭代只评估固定比例的交互，通过动态地将计算路由到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上（而非使用静态kNN图或静态掩码等固定稀疏化方法）。

Result: 在MIS问题上，推理规模扩展至基线的3倍以上，实现~8倍加速和~12倍峰值内存降低，解质量保持；在TSP问题上，n=1000时实现~15倍加速和44倍内存降低，巡游质量有竞争力；对拓扑转移具有零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRe通过动态交互预算分配有效解决了扩散组合优化求解器的可扩展性问题，在多个任务上实现了显著的加速和内存降低，同时保持了解质量，并展现出跨任务泛化能力和对拓扑变化的零样本鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>
